The months, the autumns, that is, the years, and the days make this Indra grow. **NOTE**

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- यं इंद्रं (*yaṃ iṃdraṃ*)—the Indra whom
- यज्ञं (*yajñaṃ*)—the sacrifice
- वर्धात् (*vardhāt*)—makes strong
- उत (*uta*)—and
- सोमः (*somaḥ*)—Soma
- ब्रह्म (*brahma*)—and the food in the form of oblation
- वर्धात् (*vardhāt*)—make full-grown
- गिरः (*giraḥ*)—the praises
- मन्म (*manma*)—which are means of meditation
- उक्ता (*uktā*)—and the recited mantras, make whom strong
- एनं (*enaṃ*)—that same Indra
- अक्टोः (*akṭoḥ*)—of the night
- यामन् (*yāman*)—in the time of its passing
- उषसः (*uṣasaḥ*)—the Dawns
- वर्ध (*vardha*)—make grow
- मासाः (*māsāḥ*)—the months
- शरदः (*śaradaḥ*)—the years
- द्यावः (*dyāvaḥ*)—the days
- इंद्रं (*iṃdraṃ*)—Indra
- वर्धान् (*vardhān*)—make grow

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

The Indra whom the sacrifice, the Soma that is its means, and the food in the form of oblation make full-grown, and whom the praises and the recited mantras that serve meditation make strong: that same Indra the Dawns make grow in the time of the night's passing. The months, the years and the days are causes of Indra's growth.

### Printed p. 71

##### English translation
Indra, whom the sacrifice, whom the libation exalts, whom the oblation, the praises, the prayers, the adoration exalt, whom the course of day and night exalts, whom months, and years, and days exalt.

#### Ṛk 5

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> ए॒वा ज॑ज्ञा॒नं सह॑से॒ असा॑मि वावृधा॒नं राध॑से च श्रु॒ताय॑।  
> म॒हामु॒ग्रमव॑से विप्र नू॒नमा वि॑वासेनु वृत्र॒तूर्ये॑षु ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> एव । जज्ञानं । सहसे । आसामि । ववृधानं । राधसे । च । श्रुताय ।  
> महां । उग्रं । अवसे । विप्र । नूनं । आ । विवासेम । वृत्रतूर्येषु ॥ ५ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हॆः विप्र मेधाविन्निंद्र एवैवं जज्ञानं प्रादुर्भवंतं सहसे शत्रूणामभिभवार्थमसामि बहुलं वावृधानं वर्धमानं महां महांतमुग्रमुद्गूर्णबलं
> त्वां नूनमद्य वृत्रतूर्येषु श्रुताय विश्रुताय राधसे धनाय चावसे रक्षणाय
> चा विवासेम । परिचरेम ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O wise (विप्र (*vipra*), intelligent) Indra, you who are thus (एव (*eva*)) being born, manifesting yourself (जज्ञानं (*jajñānaṃ*)), for the overcoming of enemies (सहसे (*sahase*)), असामि (*asāmi*), abundantly, growing (वावृधानं (*vāvṛdhānaṃ*)), great (महां (*mahāṃ*)), mighty (उग्रं (*ugraṃ*), of uplifted strength): you do we serve (विवासेम (*vivāsema*), परिचरेम (*paricarema*)) today in the battles in which Vṛtra is slain (वृत्रतूर्येषु (*vṛtratūryeṣu*)), for renowned (श्रुताय (*śrutāya*)) wealth (राधसे (*rādhase*)) and for protection (अवसे (*avase*)).

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- विप्र (*vipra*)—O wise Indra
- एव (*eva*)—in this manner
- जज्ञानं (*jajñānaṃ*)—one who has come into being
- सहसे (*sahase*)—to defeat the enemies
- आसामि (*āsāmi*)—exceedingly
- वावृधानं (*vāvṛdhānaṃ*)—one who has grown
- महां (*mahāṃ*)—and a great soul
- उग्रं (*ugraṃ*)—and one of fearful strength, you
- नूनं (*nūnaṃ*)—now
- वृत्रशूरेषु (*vṛtraśūreṣu*)—in the battles
- श्रुताय (*śrutāya*)—for fame
- राधसे (*rādhase*)—for wealth
- अवसे (*avase*)—for protection
- आ विवासेम (*ā vivāsema*)—we worship

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O wise Indra, you who have thus come into being, who have grown exceedingly in order to defeat the enemies, a great soul, one of fearful strength, we worship in the battles, for fame, for wealth and for protection.

### Printed p. 72

##### English translation
So, wise Indra, may we to-day propitiate you who are manifested. to overcome (our foes), you who are greatly augmenting, mightly and free, for (the sake of) wealth, fame, and protection, and for the destruction of (our) enemies.

## Sūkta 39 (Maṇḍala 6)

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> मंद्रस्य कवेरिति पंचर्चं षोडशं सूक्तं भरद्वाजस्यार્ષं त्रैष्टुभमैंद्रं ।
> मंद्रस्येत्यनुक्रांतं ॥ गतो विनियोगः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The sūkta beginning मंद्रस्य कवेरिति (*maṃdrasya kaveriti*) has five Ṛks and is the sixteenth sūkta. Its seer is Bharadvāja (Ārṣa), its metre is Triṣṭubh, and its deity is Indra. It is listed in the Anukramaṇī with the word मंद्रस्येत्यनुक्रांतं (*maṃdrasyetyanukrāṃtaṃ*). Its use has already been stated.

##### Anuvāda

This sūkta beginning "मंद्रस्य कवेः (*maṃdrasya kaveḥ*)" is the sixteenth sūkta in the third anuvāka. It has five Ṛks. Bharadvāja is the seer of this sūkta, Indra is the deity, and the metre is Triṣṭup. In the Anukramaṇikā it is said to begin with "Mandrasya". The use has been stated before.

##### Particulars

- Sūkta 39
- Maṇḍala 6; Anuvāka 3; Sūkta 39
- Aṣṭaka 4; Adhyāya 7; Varga 11
- Number of Ṛks in the sūkta: 5
- Ṛṣi: Bharadvāja Bārhaspatya
- Devatā: Indra
- Chandas: Triṣṭup

#### Ṛk 1

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> मं॒द्रस्य॑ क॒वेर्दि॒व्यस्य॒ वह्ने॒र्विप्र॑मन्मनो वच॒नस्य॒ मध्वः॑ ।  
> अपा॑ न॒स्तस्य॑ सच॒नस्य॑ दे॒वेषो॑ युवस्व गृण॒ते गोअ॑ग्राः ॥ १ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> मंद्रस्य । कवेः । दिव्यस्य । वह्नेः । विप्रमन्मनः । वचनस्य । मध्वः ।  
> अपाः । नः । तस्य । सचनस्य । देव । इषः । युवस्व । गृणते । गोऽअग्राः ॥ १ i

### Printed p. 73

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> मंद्रस्य मोदनस्य मदकरस्य वा कवेर्विक्रांतस्य दिव्यस्य दिवि भवस्य
> वह्नेर्वोढुर्विप्रमन्मनः । विप्रा मेधाविनो मन्मनः स्तोतारो यस्य स तथोक्तः ।
> तस्य वचनस्य वचनीयस्य स्तुत्यस्य तस्य सर्वत्र प्रसिद्धस्य सचनस्य सेव्यस्य एवंभूतस्य
> मध्वो मधुनः सोमस्य ॥ क्रियाग्रहणं कर्तव्यमिति कर्मणः संप्रदानत्वाच्चतुर्थ्यर्थे षष्ठी ॥ एवंभूतं नोऽस्मदीयं सोमं हे इंद्र त्वमपाः । पिब । अपि
> च हे देव द्योतमानेंद्र गृणते स्तुवते ॥ तृतीयार्थे चतुर्थी ॥ स्तुवता
> मया गोअग्राः । गावोऽग्री प्रमुखे यासां तादृशा इषोऽन्नानि युवस्व ।
> संयोजय ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

मंद्रस्य (*maṃdrasya*) is delighting or intoxicating; कवेः (*kaveḥ*), the powerful; दिव्यस्य (*divyasya*), born in heaven; वह्नेः (*vahneḥ*), the bearer; विप्रमन्मनः (*vipramanmanaḥ*) is one whose praisers (मन्मनः (*manmanaḥ*)) are the wise (विप्रा (*viprā*)); वचनस्य (*vacanasya*), that which is to be spoken of, to be praised; that which is famed everywhere; सचनस्य (*sacanasya*), to be served; the sweet Soma (मध्वो मधुनः सोमस्य (*madhvo madhunaḥ somasya*)) of such a kind, our Soma, O Indra, you drink (अपाः (*apāḥ*), पिब (*piba*)). The genitive here stands in the sense of the dative, since the act of taking is to be performed on it as the thing given. And, O shining god Indra, to me who praise (गृणते (*gṛṇate*); the dative stands for the instrumental), join (युवस्व (*yuvasva*), संयोजय (*saṃyojaya*)) foods (इषः (*iṣaḥ*), foods) that have cattle at their head. **NOTE**

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- नः (*naḥ*)—offered by us
- मंद्रस्य (*maṃdrasya*)—intoxicating
- कवेः (*kaveḥ*)—life-giving
- दिव्यस्य (*divyasya*)—born in the heavenly world
- वह्नेः (*vahneḥ*)—bearing fruit
- विप्रमन्मनः (*vipramanmanaḥ*)—meditated on by the praisers
- वचनस्य (*vacanasya*)—praiseworthy
- तस्य (*tasya*)—well known
- सचनस्य (*sacanasya*)—fit to be served
- मध्यः (*madhyaḥ*)—sweet Soma juice
- अपाः (*apāḥ*)—drink
- देव (*deva*)—O shining Indra
- गृणते (*gṛṇate*)—with me, the praiser
- गो अग्राः (*go agrāḥ*)—having cattle as the chief part
- इषः (*iṣaḥ*)—foods
- युवस्व (*yuvasva*)—join

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, drink the sweet Soma juice, offered by us, intoxicating, life-giving, born in the heavenly world, bearing fruit, meditated on by the praisers, praiseworthy, well known and fit to be served. O shining Indra, join to me, the praiser, foods that have cattle as their chief part; that is, fill me with all of them.

##### English translation
Drink, Indra, of that our sweet, exhilarating, inspiring, celestial, fruit-yielding Soma, commended by the wise, and entitled to praise and preparation: bestow upon him who glorifies you, divine (Indra), food, the chiefest of which is cattle.

#### Ṛk 2

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अ॒यमु॑शा॒नः पयद्रि॑मु॒स्रा ऋ॒तधी॑तिभिर्ऋत॒युग्यु॑जा॒नः ।  
> रु॒जदरु॑ग्णं॒ वि व॒लस्य॒ सानुं॑ प॒णीर्वचो॑भिर॒भि यो॑ध॒दिंद्रः॑ ॥

### Printed p. 74

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अयं । उशानः । परि । अद्रं । उस्राः । ऋतधीतिभिः । ऋत्ययुक् । युजानः ।  
> रुजत् । अरुग्णं । वि । वलस्य । सानुं । पणीन् । वचःभिः । अभि । योधत् । इंद्रः ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> आयमिंद्रोऽद्रिं वलेन गोपिधानाय निर्मितं शिलोच्चयं परि परितः
> पर्वतसमीपे विद्यमाना उस्रा गा उशानः कामयमान ऋतधीतिभिः सत्यकर्मभिरंगिरोभिर्युजानः संगच्छमान ऋतयुक् ऋतेन तत्कृतेन स्तोत्रेण युक्तश्च
> भूत्वा वलस्यासुरस्य स्वभूतं सानुमुच्च्रितं पर्वतमरुग्णमन्मैरभग्नं वि रुजत् ।
> व्यभांक्षीत् । किं च पणीन्वलानुचरानेतत्संज्ञानसुरांश्च वचोभिः स्तुत्यैरायुध्यैः
> संतर्जनरूपैर्वाक्यैरेव वायमिंद्रोऽभि योधत् । अभ्ययुध्यत् ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

This Indra, desiring (उशानः (*uśānaḥ*)) the cows (उस्रा (*usrā*)) that were near the mountain (अद्रिं (*adriṃ*)), the mass of rock made by Vala to enclose the cows, joining (युजानः (*yujānaḥ*)) the Aṅgirases whose acts were true (ऋतधीतिभिः (*ṛtadhītibhiḥ*)), and being joined with the praise (ऋतयुक् (*ṛtayuk*)) made by them, broke (वि रुजत् (*vi rujat*)) the unbroken (अरुग्णं (*arugṇaṃ*)) summit (सानुं (*sānuṃ*)), the high mountain belonging to the asura Vala. And further, the Paṇis, the asuras of that name who were Vala's followers, Indra fought (अभि योधत् (*abhi yodhat*)) with words alone (वचोभिः (*vacobhiḥ*)), words that were praiseworthy and warlike, in the nature of संतर्जन (*saṃtarjana*).

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अयं इंद्रः (*ayaṃ iṃdraḥ*)—this Indra
- अद्रिं परि (*adriṃ pari*)—around the mountain
- उस्राः (*usrāḥ*)—the cows
- उशानः (*uśānaḥ*)—wishing to obtain
- यतधीतिभिः (*yatadhītibhiḥ*)—with the Aṅgirases, whose acts are true
- युजानः (*yujānaḥ*)—joining
- यतयुक् (*yatayuk*)—being furnished with praise
- वलस्य (*valasya*)—of the asura Vala
- अरुग्णं (*arugṇaṃ*)—which others could not enter
- सानुं (*sānuṃ*)—the high part of the mountain
- वि रुजत् (*vi rujat*)—split apart
- पणीन् (*paṇīn*)—the Paṇis, followers of Vala
- वचोभिः (*vacobhiḥ*)—with harsh words alone
- अभि योधत् (*abhi yodhat*)—he confronted

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

Wishing to obtain the cows that were around the mountain made by Vala to confine the cows, Indra joined the Aṅgirases, whose acts are true, and, furnished with praise, split apart the high part of the mountain of the asura Vala, which others could not enter. He confronted the Paṇis, the followers of Vala, with harsh words alone.

##### English translation
Determined (to recover) the cattle hidden in the mountain, associated with the celebrators of pure rites, (the Angirasas), and animated by (their) veracious (praise), this (Indra) fractured the infrangible rock of Vala. and overwhelmed the Panis with reproaches.

### Printed p. 75

#### Ṛk 3

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अ॒यं द्यो॑तयद॒द्युतो॒ व्य१॒ं॑ कून्दो॒षा वस्तोः॑ श॒रद॒  
> इंदु॑रिंद्र ।  
> इ॒मं के॒तुम॑दधु॒र्नू चि॒दह्नां॒ शुचि॑जन्मन उ॒षस॑श्चकार ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अयं । द्योतयत् । अद्युतः । वि । अक्तून् । दोषा । वस्तोः । शरदः । इंदुः ।  
> इंद्र ।  
> इमं । केतुं । अदधुः । नु । चित् । आह्नां । शुचिजन्मनः । उषसः । चकार ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे इंद्र त्वया नियम्यमानः पीयमानो वायमिंदुः सोमोऽद्युतोऽद्योतमानानक्तून् । अक्तुरिति रात्रिनाम । अत्र च तत्संबंधात्पक्षमासादयो
> लक्ष्यंते । पक्षमासादीन् तथा दोषा रात्रिं वसोर्दिवसं शरदः संवत्सरांश्च वि
> द्योतयत् । व्यदीपयत् । कुत इत्यत आह । नू चित् पुरा देवा इमं सोमं
> चंद्रात्मना नभसि वर्तमानमह्नां दिवसानां केतुं प्रज्ञापकं यस्माददधुः
> व्यदधुः अकुर्वन् तस्मादनेन पक्षमासादयः सर्वे प्रकाश्यंते । चंद्रगत्यधीनत्वात्तिथिविभागानां । अपि चायं सोम उषस उषःकालान् शुचिजन्मन आत्मीयेन
> तेजसा शुद्धोदयान् चकार । करोति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Indra, this Indu, Soma, controlled (नियम्यमानः (*niyamyamānaḥ*)) or drunk by you, illumined (वि द्योतयत् (*vi dyotayat*)) the dark (अद्युतः (*adyutaḥ*)) अक्तून् (*aktūn*). अक्तुरिति (*akturiti*) is a name of night; here, by association with it, fortnights, months and the like are meant. So Soma illumined the fortnights, months and the like, the night (दोषा (*doṣā*)), the day (वस्तोः (*vastoḥ*)), the autumns, that is, the years. Why? Because the gods formerly (नू चित् (*nū cit*)) set up (अदधुः (*adadhuḥ*)) this Soma, which stands in the sky in the form of the moon, as the indicator (केतुं (*ketuṃ*)) of the days (अह्नां (*ahnāṃ*)), and therefore by it all fortnights, months and the like are made known, since the divisions of the lunar days depend on the moon's course. Moreover this Soma makes the dawns (उषसः (*uṣasaḥ*)), the times of dawn, of pure birth (शुचिजन्मन (*śucijanmana*)), that is, of pure rising, by its own radiance.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- इंद्र (*iṃdra*)—O Indra
- नू चित् (*nū cit*)—formerly
- इमं (*imaṃ*)—this Soma
- अह्नां (*ahnāṃ*)—for the days
- केतुं (*ketuṃ*)—as the indicator (in the form of the moon)
- अदधुः (*adadhuḥ*)—(the gods) established
- आयं इंदुः (*āyaṃ iṃduḥ*)—this Soma, therefore
- अद्युतः (*adyutaḥ*)—which are without light
- अक्तून् (*aktūn*)—of the dark fortnights and the like
- दोषा (*doṣā*)—the nights
- वस्तोः (*vastoḥ*)—the days
- शरदः (*śaradaḥ*)—the years
- वि द्योतयत् (*vi dyotayat*)—made to shine
- उषसः (*uṣasaḥ*)—the Dawns
- श.चिजन्मनः (*śa.cijanmanaḥ*)—of purified birth
- चकार (*cakāra*)—made

### Printed p. 76

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, in former times the gods established this Soma as the indicator, in the form of the moon, for the days. Therefore this Soma made to shine the nights of the dark fortnights and the like, which are without light, and the days and the years. He made the births of the Dawns pure.

##### English translation
This Soma, Indra, (quaffed by you), has lighted up the unlustrous nights, and days and nights, and years : (the gods) of old have established it as the ensign of days, and it has made the dawns generated in light.

#### Ṛk 4

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अ॒यं रो॑चयद॒रुचो॑ रुचा॒नो॒३॒॑ ३ यं वा॑सय॒द्व्य १ १॒॑तेन॑  ॒
> पू॒र्वीः । ॒ 
> अ॒यमी॑यत ऋत॒युग्भि॒रश्वैः॑ स्व॒र्विदा॒ नाभि॑ना चर्षणि॒प्राः ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अयं । रोचयत् । अरुचः । रुचानः । अयं । वासयत् । वि । ऋतेन । पूर्वीः ।  
> अयं । ईयते । ऋतयुक्भिः । अश्वैः । स्वःविदा । नाभिना । चर्षणिप्राः ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> अयमिंद्रो रुचानो रोचमानः सूर्यात्मना दीप्यमानोऽरुचः
> प्रकाशरहितान् तमसा मलीमसान् सर्वाल्लोकान्नोचयत् । प्रकाशयति । तथा
> पूर्वीर्बहीरुषसश्चायमिंद्र ऋतेन सत्यभूतेन सर्वत्र गमनशीलेन वा तेजसा
> वि वासयत् । व्यापयति । तत्रत्यानि तमांसि निवर्तयतीत्यर्थः । तथा ऋतयुग्भिऋतेन स्तोत्रेण युज्यमानैरश्वैर्नाभिना संनद्देन स्वर्विदा सुष्वरणीयस्य
> धनस्य लंभकेन रथेन चर्षणिप्राश्चर्यणीनां मनुष्याणां कामैः पूरयिता सन्
> अयमेवेंद्र ईयते । गच्छति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

This Indra, shining (रुचानो (*rucāno*)) in the form of the Sun, illumines all the worlds that are without light, made dark by gloom. Likewise this Indra pervades the many dawns with ऋतेन (*ṛtena*), that is, with a radiance that is true in nature or that moves everywhere, and thereby removes the darkness in them. Likewise, being the fulfiller of the desires of men, he moves (ईयते (*īyate*)) with horses yoked by praise (ṛta here meaning praise), on a chariot that is well fastened and that obtains wealth worthy of being well proclaimed.

### Printed p. 77

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अयं (*ayaṃ*)—this Indra
- रुचानः (*rucānaḥ*)—shining in the form of the Sun
- अरुचः (*arucaḥ*)—all the worlds that are without light
- रोचयत् (*rocayat*)—he illumines
- पूर्विः (*pūrviḥ*)—the many dawns
- आयं (*āyaṃ*)—this Indra
- ऋतेन (*ṛtena*)—with his own lustre of the nature of truth
- वि वासयत् (*vi vāsayat*)—has pervaded
- ऋतयुग्भिः (*ṛtayugbhiḥ*)—endowed with praise
- अश्वैः (*aśvaiḥ*)—by horses
- नाभिना (*nābhinā*)—fastened
- स्वर्विदा (*svarvidā*)—and rich in wealth, along with the chariot
- चर्षणिप्राः (*carṣaṇiprāḥ*)—as the fulfiller of the desires of all men
- अयं (*ayaṃ*)—this Indra
- ईयते (*īyate*)—moves about

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

This same Indra, shining in the form of the Sun, illumines all the worlds that are without light. This Indra has pervaded the many dawns with his own lustre of the nature of truth. With a chariot that is fastened and rich in wealth, drawn by horses endowed with praise, Indra moves about as the fulfiller of the desires of all men.

##### English translation
This radiant (Indra) has illumed the non-radiant (worlds): he has pervaded many dawns with true lustre: the benefactor of men moves in (a chariot) drawn by horses, harnessed by praise, laden with riches.

#### Ṛk 5

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> नू गृ॑णा॒नो गृ॑ण॒ते प्र॑त्न राज॒न्निषः॑ पिन्व वसु॒देया॑य  ॒
> पू॒र्वीः । ॒ 
> अ॒प ओष॑धीरवि॒षा वना॑नि॒ गा अर्व॑तो॒ नृ॒न्नचसे॑ रिरीहि ॥ ५ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> नु । गृणानः । गृणते । प्रश्नॆ । राजन् । इषः । पिन्न । वसुदेयाय । पूर्वीः ।  
> अपः । ओषधीः । अविषा । वनानि । गाः । अर्वतः । नृन् । ऋचसे । रिरीहि ॥ ५ ॥

### Printed p. 78

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे प्रश्नॆ पुरातन राजन् राजमानेंद्र गृणानः स्तूयमानस्त्वं वसुदेयाय
> वसूनि धनानि त्वया देयानि यस्मै तादृशाय गृणते स्तुवते पूर्वीर्बह्वरिषोऽन्नानि नु क्षिप्रं पिन्व । सिंच । प्रयच्छेत्यर्थः । किं च ऋचसेऽर्चयित्रे
> स्तोत्रेऽपो वृष्टिलक्षणान्युदकान्नोषधीर्वीह्यादीन् अविषा विषरहितानि रक्षकाणि
> वा वनानि वृक्षजातानि चूतपनसादीनि गा अर्वतोऽश्वानन्मनुष्यांश्च कर्मकर्मान्
> रिरीहि । देहि ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O ancient and shining Indra, being praised, you should quickly bestow (पिन्व (*pinva*), meaning sprinkle, give) many foods on the praiser, one to whom riches are to be given by you. Moreover, to the worshipper, the praiser, give the waters in the form of rain, the plants such as rice, the trees such as mango and jackfruit, which are free of poison or protective, and cows, horses and men who do work.**NOTE**

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- प्रश्न (*praśna*)—O ancient one
- राजन् (*rājan*)—O shining Indra
- गृणानः (*gṛṇānaḥ*)—you who are to be praised
- वसुदेयाय (*vasudeyāya*)—to him to whom wealth is to be given
- गृणते (*gṛṇate*)—to the praiser
- पूर्विः (*pūrviḥ*)—of many kinds
- इषः (*iṣaḥ*)—foods
- नु (*nu*)—quickly
- पिन्व (*pinva*)—provide
- ऋचसे (*ṛcase*)—to your worshipper
- अपः (*apaḥ*)—rain-waters
- ओषधीः (*oṣadhīḥ*)—rice and the like
- अविषा (*aviṣā*)—free of poison
- वनानि (*vanāni*)—trees
- गाः (*gāḥ*)—cows
- आर्वतः (*ārvataḥ*)—horses
- नृन् (*nṛn*)—sons, servants and the like
- रिरीहि (*rirīhi*)—provide

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, ancient and shining, you who are to be praised, quickly provide many kinds of food to the praiser, to whom you are to give wealth. To your worshipper provide rain-waters, rice and the like, trees free of poison, cows, horses, and sons, servants and the like.

##### English translation
Sovereign of old, when glorified, you bestow upon him who praises you, and to whom affluence is due, abundant food: grant to the worshipper water, plants, innocuous woods, cattle, horses, and men.

## Sūkta 40 (Maṇḍala 6)

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> इंद्र पिबेति पंचर्चं सप्तदशं सूक्तं भरद्वाजस्यार्षं त्रैष्टुभमैंद्रं ।
> इंद्र पिबेत्यनुक्रांतं ॥ गतः सूक्तविनियोगः ॥ प्रथमे रात्रिपर्यायेच्छावाकस्याद्या शस्त्रयाज्या । सूत्रितं च । इंद्र पिब तुभ्यं सुतो मदायेति याज्या
> आ. ६-४ ॥ इति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The sūkta beginning इंद्र पिबेति (*iṃdra pibeti*) has five Ṛks and is the seventeenth sūkta. Its seer is Bharadvāja (Ārṣa), its metre is Triṣṭubh, and its deity is Indra. It is listed in the Anukramaṇī with the words इंद्र पिबेत्यनुक्रांतं (*iṃdra pibetyanukrāṃtaṃ*). The use of the sūkta has already been stated. In the first night-round the first Ṛk is the śastra-yājyā of the Acchāvāka priest. It is stated in the sūtra: इंद्र पिब तुभ्यं सुतो मदायेति याज्या (*iṃdra piba tubhyaṃ suto madāyeti yājyā*) (Ā. Sū. 6-4).

### Printed p. 79

##### Anuvāda

This sūkta beginning "Indra piba" is the seventeenth sūkta in the third anuvāka. It has five Ṛks. Bharadvāja is the seer of this sūkta, Indra is the deity, and the metre is Triṣṭup. In the Anukramaṇikā it is said to begin with "Indra piba". The use has been stated before. And at the time of reciting the first Chandomā mantras, in the night-round, the priest called Acchāvāka is to recite the first Ṛk of this sūkta as the śastra-yājyā mantra, as is explained by the sūtra of the Āśvalāyana Śrauta Sūtra, "Indra piba tubhyaṃ suto madāyeti yājyā" (Ā. 6-4).

##### Particulars

- Sūkta 40
- Maṇḍala 6; Anuvāka 3; Sūkta 40
- Aṣṭaka 4; Adhyāya 7; Varga 12
- Number of Ṛks in the sūkta: 5
- Ṛṣi: Bharadvāja Bārhaspatya
- Devatā: Indra
- Chandas: Triṣṭup

#### Ṛk 1

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> इंद्र॒ पिब॒ तुभ्यं॑ सु॒तो मदा॒याव॑ स्य॒ हरी॒ वि मु॑चा॒  ॑
> सखाया ।  
> उ॒त प्र गा॑य ग॒ण आ नि॒षद्याथा॑ य॒ज्ञाय॑ गृण॒ते वयो॑ धाः॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> इंद्र । पिब । तुभ्यं । सुतः । मदाय । अव । स्य । हरी इति । वि । मुच । सखाया ।  
> उत । प्र । गाय । गणे । आ । निसद्य । अथ । यज्ञाय । गृणते । वयः । धाः ॥ १ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे इंद्र तं सोमं पिब यः सोमस्तुभ्यं मदाय मदार्थं
> सुतोऽभिषुतः । यतस्त्वया सोमः पातव्योऽतः कारणात्सखाया समानख्यान्
> मित्रभूतौ वा हरी अश्वावव स्य । अवस्थापय । तदनंतरं वि मुच । रथाद्विमुंच ।

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Indra, drink that Soma which has been pressed for you for your exhilaration. Since the Soma is to be drunk by you, for that reason make your two horses, who are your friends or bear the same name, stand still (अव स्य (*ava sya*)), and afterwards unyoke them from the chariot.

### Printed p. 80

> उतापि च गणेऽस्मत्सोतृसंघ आ अभिमुख्येन निषद्योपविश्य प्र गाय । आस्माभिः
> कृतं स्तोत्रमुपश्लोकय ! अथानंतरं यज्ञाय यजमानाय गृणते स्तुवते
> वयोऽन्नं धाः । देहि ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

And also, sitting down facing us in this company of us Soma-pressers, sing (प्र गाय (*pra gāya*)), that is, listen to the hymn made by us. Then give food to the sacrificer who praises.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- इंद्र (*iṃdra*)—O Indra
- तुभ्यं (*tubhyaṃ*)—for you
- मदाय (*madāya*)—to cause you delight
- सुतः (*sutaḥ*)—the Soma juice that has been pressed, that
- पिब (*piba*)—drink
- सखाया (*sakhāyā*)—your two friendly
- हरी (*harī*)—horses
- अव स्य (*ava sya*)—make stand
- वि मुच (*vi muca*)—(afterwards) unyoke them
- गणे (*gaṇe*)—in our company
- अथ (*atha*)—afterwards
- गृणते (*gṛṇate*)—to the praiser
- वयः (*vayaḥ*)—food
- धाः (*dhāḥ*)—give

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, drink the Soma that has been pressed for you, to cause you delight. Make your friendly horses stand. Afterwards unyoke them, and, sitting in our company facing us, graciously accept our praise. Afterwards give food to the praiser, the sacrificer.

##### English translation
Drink, Indra, (the Soma) that is effused for your exhilaration : stop your friendly steeds: let them loose: sitting in our society, respond to our hymns: give food to him who lauds and worships you.

#### Ṛk 2

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अस्य॑ पिब॒ यस्य॑ जज्ञा॒न इं॑द्र॒ मदा॑य॒ क्रत्वे॒ अपि॑बो  
> विरप्सिन् ।  
> तमु॑ ते॒ गावो॒ नर॒ आपो॒ अद्रि॒रिंदुं॒ सम॑ह्यन्पी॒तये॒  ॑
> समस्यॆ ॥ २ ॥

### Printed p. 81

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अस्य । पिब । यस्य । जज्ञानः । इंद्र । मदाय । कृत्वे । अपिबः । विरप्सिन् ।  
> तं। ऊं इति । ते । गावः । नरः । आपः । अद्रिः । इंदुं । सं । अह्यन् । पीतये । सं ।  
> अस्मै ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> द्वितीये. रात्रिपर्यायेच्छावाकस्यास्य पिबेति शस्त्रयाज्या । सूत्रितं च ।
> अस्य पिबेति याज्या । आ. ६-४ । इति.
> हे इंद्र अस्येमं सोमं पिब । हे विरष्यन् महन् जज्ञानो जायमान
> एव त्वं यस्य यं सोममपिबः पूर्वं पीतवानसि । किमर्थं । मदाय हर्षाय
> क्रत्वे कर्मणे वृत्रवधादिलक्षणं वीर्यकर्म कर्तुं च । तमु तादृशमेवेंदुं
> सोमं गावो गवि भवाः श्रपणसाधनाः क्षीरादयो नरो नेतारोऽध्वर्यव
> अपो वसतीवर्याख्या अद्रिरभिषवार्थो ग्रावा एते सर्वेऽस्मा अस्येंद्रस्य ते तव
> पीतये पानार्थं समह्यन् । समगमयन् ॥ हि गतावित्यस्यैतद्रूपं । पुनः
> समिति पूरकः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

In the second night-round this Ṛk अस्य पिबेति (*asya pibeti*) is the śastra-yājyā of the Acchāvāka. It is stated in the sūtra: अस्य पिबेति याज्या (*asya pibeti yājyā*) (Ā. Sū. 6-4). O Indra, drink this Soma. O mighty one, being born, you drank in former times that Soma, for exhilaration and for the deed of valour, such as the slaying of Vṛtra. Just this Soma, the cow products such as milk used for cooking, the leaders, the Adhvaryu priests, the waters called Vasatīvarī, and the pressing stones, all these have brought together for this Indra, for your drinking.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- विरप्सिन् (*virapsin*)—O great one
- इंद्र (*iṃdra*)—O Indra
- जज्ञानः (*jajñānaḥ*)—as soon as you were born
- यस्य (*yasya*)—which Soma
- मदाय (*madāya*)—for your delight
- क्रत्वे (*kratve*)—and for the deed such as the slaying of Vṛtra
- अपिबः (*apibaḥ*)—you drank
- अस्य (*asya*)—that same Soma
- पिब (*piba*)—drink
- तमु इंदुं (*tamu iṃduṃ*)—that same Soma
- गावः (*gāvaḥ*)—the milk and the like produced from cows
- नरः (*naraḥ*)—the Adhvaryus
- आपः (*āpaḥ*)—the Vasatīvarī waters and the like
- अद्रिः (*adriḥ*)—the pressing stones
- अस्मै (*asmai*)—for this one in the form of Indra
- ते (*te*)—your
- पीतये (*pītaye*)—for drinking
- समह्यन् (*samahyan*)—brought together and pressed

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O great Indra, as soon as you were born, you drank the Soma for your delight and for the deed such as the slaying of Vṛtra: drink that same Soma. The milk and the like produced from cows, the Adhvaryus, the Vasatīvarī waters and the like, and the pressing stones brought together and pressed that same Soma for your drinking, you who are Indra.

### Printed p. 82

##### English translation
Drink, Indra, of this (libation), of which you, mighty one, have drunk as soon as born, for excitement to (great) deeds; that Soma juice which the cows, the priests, the waters, the stones, combine to prepare for your drinking.

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

In the second night-round, the priest called Acchāvāka is to recite this Ṛk अस्य पिब (*asya piba*) as the śastra-yājyā mantra, as is explained by the sūtra of the Āśvalāyana Śrauta Sūtra, अस्य पिबेति याज्या (*asya pibeti yājyā*) (Ā. 6-4).

#### Ṛk 3

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> समि॑द्दे अ॒ग्नौ सु॒त इं॑द्र॒ सोम॒ आ त्वा॑ वहंतु॒ हर॑यो॒  ॑
> वहि॑ष्ठाः ।  
> त्वा॒य॒ता मन॑सा जोहवी॒मींद्रा या॑हि सुवि॒ताय॑ म॒हे  ॑
> नः ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> संइद्दे । अग्नौ । सुते । इंद्र । सोमे । आ । त्वा । वहंतु । हरयः । वहिष्ठाः ।  
> त्वायता । मनसा । जोहवीमि । इंद्र । आ । याहि । सुविताय । महे । नः ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> अग्नौ समिद्धे दीप्ते सोमे सुतेऽभिषुते सति हे इंद्र त्वा त्वां वहिष्मा
> वोढृतमा हरयोऽश्वा आ वहंतु । इमं यज्ञं प्रत्यानयंतु । अहं च त्वायता
> त्वां कामयमानेन मनसा जोहवीमि । पुनः पुनराह्वयामि । हे इंद्र त्वं
> नोऽस्माकं महे महते सुविताय कल्याणाया याहि । आगच्छ ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

When the fire is kindled and the Soma has been pressed, O Indra, let the horses that are the best carriers bring you here; let them bring you to this sacrifice. And I, with a mind that desires you, invoke you again and again. O Indra, come to our great welfare.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- इंद्र (*iṃdra*)—O Indra
- अग्नौ (*agnau*)—when the fire
- समिद्धे (*samiddhe*)—has been kindled (and)
- सोमे (*some*)—the Soma juice
- सुते (*sute*)—has been pressed
- त्वा (*tvā*)—you
- वहिष्ठाः (*vahiṣṭhāḥ*)—the best carriers

### Printed p. 83

- हरयः (*harayaḥ*)—the horses
- आ वहंतु (*ā vahaṃtu*)—let them bring to our sacrifice
- त्वायता (*tvāyatā*)—desiring only your help
- मनसा (*manasā*)—with a mind
- जोहवीमि (*johavīmi*)—I invoke you
- इंद्र (*iṃdra*)—O Indra
- नः (*naḥ*)—our
- महे (*mahe*)—great
- सुविताय (*suvitāya*)—for the welfare
- आ याहि (*ā yāhi*)—be gracious and come

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, when the fire is kindled and the Soma juice has been pressed, let the horses that are to carry you bring you to our sacrifice. With a mind that desires only your help, I invoke you again and again. O Indra, be gracious for our great welfare.

##### English translation
The fire is kindled ; the Soma, Indra, is effused : let your vigorous horses bring you hither : I invoke you, Indra, with a mind wholly devoted to you: come for our great prosperity.

#### Ṛk 4

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> आ या॑हि॒ शश्व॑दुश॒ता य॑या॒थेंद्र॑ म॒हा मन॑सा सोम॒पेयं॑।  
> उप॒ ब्रह्मा॑णि शृणव इ॒मा नोऽथा॑ य॒ज्ञस्त॒न्वे॒३॒॑३ वयो॑  
> धात् ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> आ । याहि । शश्वत् । उशता । ययाथ । इंद्र । महा । मनसा । सोमपेयं ।  
> उप । ब्रह्माणि । शृणवः । इमा । नः । अथ । ते ! यज्ञः । तन्ने । वयः । धात् ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे इंद्र पुरा शश्वद्बहुकृत्वो ययाथ । सोमपानार्थं यज्ञं गतवानसि ।
> आतः कारणादुशतास्मान्यामयमानेन महा महता मनसा सह सोमपेयं
> सोमः पीयते यस्मिन् तादृशमस्मदीयं यज्ञमा याहि । अभिगच्छ । अगत्य च
> ब्रह्माणि स्तोत्राणीमेदानीं प्रयुज्यमानानि नोऽस्मदीयान्युप शृणवः ।
> उपश्वन । अथानंतरं यज्ञो यजमानस्ते तव तन्ने शरीराय त्वच्छरीरपोषार्थं
> वयोऽन्नं सोमात्मकं धात् । ददातु ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Indra, you have gone many times in the past to the sacrifice for the drinking of Soma. For that reason, with a great mind that desires us, come to this our sacrifice, in which the Soma is drunk. Having come, hear these praises of ours that are now being recited. Afterwards may the sacrificer give you food consisting of Soma, for the nourishment of your body.

### Printed p. 84

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- इंद्र (*iṃdra*)—O Indra
- शश्वत् (*śaśvat*)—formerly, many times
- ययाथ (*yayātha*)—you have gone to such sacrifices
- उशता (*uśatā*)—desiring our company
- महा (*mahā*)—and generous
- मनसा (*manasā*)—with a mind
- सोमपेयं (*somapeyaṃ*)—to our sacrifice, which is ready for the drinking of Soma
- आ याहि (*ā yāhi*)—be gracious and come
- नः (*naḥ*)—recited by us
- इमा ब्रह्माणि (*imā brahmāṇi*)—these praises
- उप शृणवः (*upa śṛṇavaḥ*)—hear
- अथ (*atha*)—afterwards
- यज्ञः (*yajñaḥ*)—the sacrificer
- ते (*te*)—your
- तन्ने (*tanne*)—for the nourishment of the body
- वयः (*vayaḥ*)—food in the form of Soma
- धात् (*dhāt*)—may he offer

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, you have gone many times in the past to such sacrifices. With a mind that desires our company and is generous, be gracious and come to our sacrifice, which is ready for the drinking of Soma. Hear these praises recited by us. Afterwards may the sacrificer offer you food in the form of Soma, for the nourishment of your body.

##### English translation
You have ever gone (to similar rites): come now with a great mind disposed to drink the Soma: hear these our praises: may the worshipper present to you (sacrificial) food for (the nourishment of) your person.

#### Ṛk 5

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> यदिं॑द्र दि॒वि पार्ये॒ यदृध॒ग्यद्वा॒ स्वे सद॑ने॒ यत्र॒ वासि॑ ।  
> अतो॑ नो य॒ज्ञमव॑से नि॒युत्वा॑न्त्स॒जोषाः॑ पाहि गिर्वणो  ॒॑
> मरुद्भिः ॥ ५ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> यत् । इंद्र । दिवि । पाये । यत् । यधक् । यत् । वा । स्वे । सदने । यत् ।  
> वा । असि ।  
> अतः । नः । यज्ञं । अवसे । नियुत्वान् । सजोषाः । पाहि । गिर्वणः । मरुत्भिः॥

### Printed p. 85

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे इंद्र पाये पारगे दूरदेशे दिवि द्युलोकाख्ये यद्यदि त्वं वर्तसे ।
> यदि वा ऋधक् । एतत्पृथक्क्येन समानार्थं । पृथगन्यस्मिन्देशे वर्तसे । यद्वा
> स्वे स्वकीये सदने गृहे । अथवा किमनेन विशेषणेन यत्र यस्मिन्देशेऽसि भवसि
> अतोऽस्मात्स्थानादागत्य हे गिर्वणो गिरां संभक्तरिंद्र नियुत्वान् । नियुतोऽश्वाः ।
> तद्वान्मरुद्भिः सजोषाः सह प्रीयमाणस्त्वं नोऽस्माकमवसे रक्षणाय यज्ञमस्मदीयं यागं पाहि । रक्ष । यद्वा । यज्ञं यजनसाधनमस्मदीयं सोममवसे
> तर्पणाय पाहि । पिब ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Indra, if you are in the far-off region called heaven, or else (ऋधक् (*ṛdhak*), which has the same sense as पृथक् (*pṛthak*)) apart, in another region, or in your own abode, or, what is the use of this specifying, in whatever place you are, then coming from that place, O Girvaṇas, lover of songs, O Indra, accompanied by your horses (नियुतोऽश्वाः (*niyuto 'śvāḥ*)), well pleased together with the Maruts, protect our sacrifice for our protection. Or else: drink our Soma, the means of sacrifice, for your satisfaction.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- गिर्वणः (*girvaṇaḥ*)—O you who accept praise
- इंद्र (*iṃdra*)—O Indra
- यत् (*yat*)—if perchance (you)
- पारै (*pārai*)—are in a far-off region
- दिवि (*divi*)—or in heaven
- यद्वा (*yadvā*)—or
- स्वे सदने (*sve sadane*)—in your own house
- यत्र वा असि (*yatra vā asi*)—or in whatever region you may be
- आतः (*ātaḥ*)—from that region
- नियुत्वान् (*niyutvān*)—yoked with horses
- मरुद्भिः (*marudbhiḥ*)—with the Maruts
- सजोषाः (*sajoṣāḥ*)—and joined
- नः (*naḥ*)—our
- अवसे (*avase*)—for protection
- यज्ञं (*yajñaṃ*)—our sacrifice
- पाहि (*pāhi*)—protect

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, you who accept praise, whether you are in a far-off region, in heaven, or in your own house, in whatever region you may be, come from that region, yoked with horses and joined with the Maruts, and protect our sacrifice, which is the means of our protection.

##### English translation
Whether, Indra, you abide in the distant heaven, in any other place, or in your own abode, or wherever (you may be), from thence do you, who are propitiated by praise, putting to your steeds, protect, together with the Maruts, well pleased, our sacrifice, for our preservation.

### Printed p. 86

## Sūkta 41 (Maṇḍala 6)

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> अहेळमान इति पंचर्चमष्टादशं सूक्तं भरद्वाजस्यार्षं त्रैष्टुभमैंद्रं ।
> अनुक्रांतं च । अहेळमान इति ॥ गतो विनियोगः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The sūkta beginning अहेळमान इति (*aheḷamāna iti*) has five Ṛks and is the eighteenth sūkta. Its seer is Bharadvāja (Ārṣa), its metre is Triṣṭubh, and its deity is Indra. It is listed in the Anukramaṇī with the words अहेळमान इति (*aheḷamāna iti*). The use has already been stated.

##### Anuvāda

This sūkta beginning "अहेळमानः (*aheḷamānaḥ*)" is the eighteenth sūkta in the third anuvāka. It has five Ṛks. Bharadvāja is the seer of this sūkta, Indra is the deity, and the metre is Triṣṭup. In the Anukramaṇikā it is said to begin with "अहेळमानः (*aheḷamānaḥ*)". The use has been stated before.

##### Particulars

- Sūkta 41
- Maṇḍala 6; Anuvāka 3; Sūkta 41
- Aṣṭaka 4; Adhyāya 7; Varga 13
- Number of Ṛks in the sūkta: 5
- Ṛṣi: Bharadvāja Bārhaspatya
- Devatā: Indra
- Chandas: Triṣṭup

#### Ṛk 1

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अहे॑ळमान॒ उप॑ याहि य॒ज्ञं तुभ्यं॑ पवंत॒ इंद॑वः सु॒तासः॑।  
> गावो॒ न व॑ज्रि॒न्त्वमोको॒ अच्छेंद्रा ग॑हि प्रथ॒मो य॒ज्ञिया॑नां ॥ १ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अहेळमानः । उप । याहि । यज्ञं । तुभ्यं । पवंते । इंदवः । सुतासः !  
> गावः । न । वज्रिन् । स्वं । ओकः । अच्छ । इंद्र । आ । गहि । प्रथमः । यज्जियानां ॥१॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे इंद्र अहेळमानोऽक्रुध्यंत्वं यज्ञमस्मदीयमुप याहि । उपगच्छ ।
> यतः कारणात्तुभ्यं त्वदर्थं सुतासोऽभिषुता इंदवः सोमाः पवंते पूयंते

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Indra, free from anger, come to our sacrifice, since for you the pressed Soma juices are purified

### Printed p. 87

> दशापवित्रेण शोध्यंते । हे वज्रिन् वज्रवन्निंद्र गावो न गावः स्वकीयं गोष्ठमिव
> स्वं स्वकीयमोकः स्थानं द्रोणकलशादिलक्षणमच्छाभिमुख्येन । प्राप्नुवंतीति
> शेषः । अतः कारणात् हे इंद्र यज्ञियानां यज्ञार्हाणां देवानां मध्यॆ
> प्रथमो मुख्यस्त्वमा गहि । आगच्छ ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

with the ten-stranded filter. O thunderbolt-bearer, as cows go to their own stall, so the Soma juices go to their own place, such as the Droṇa vessel, facing it (the word to be supplied is: they reach). Therefore, O Indra, you who are the first, the chief, among the gods worthy of sacrifice, come.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अहेळमानः (*aheḷamānaḥ*)—O Indra, free from anger
- यज्ञं (*yajñaṃ*)—to our sacrifice
- उप याहि (*upa yāhi*)—be gracious and come
- तुभ्यं (*tubhyaṃ*)—directed to you
- सुतासः (*sutāsaḥ*)—pressed
- इंदवः (*iṃdavaḥ*)—the Soma juices
- पवंते (*pavaṃte*)—are purified
- वज्रन् (*vajran*)—O Indra, bearer of the thunderbolt
- गावो न (*gāvo na*)—as cows go with eagerness to their stall
- स्वं (*svaṃ*)—their own
- ओकः (*okaḥ*)—place in the form of the Droṇa vessel
- अच्छ (*accha*)—with eagerness (these Soma juices) go and reach
- इंद्र (*iṃdra*)—O Indra
- यज्ञयानां (*yajñayānāṃ*)—among the gods worthy of sacrifice
- प्रथमः (*prathamaḥ*)—you, the chief
- आ गहि (*ā gahi*)—be gracious and come

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, free from anger, be gracious and come to our sacrifice. The Soma juices pressed for you are purified. O Indra, bearer of the thunderbolt, as cows go with eagerness to their stall, the Soma juices reach and fill their own vessel in the form of the Droṇa vessel. O Indra, you who are the chief among the gods worthy of sacrifice, be gracious and come.

##### English translation
Unirascible (Indra), come to the sacrifice : the effused juices are purified for you : they flow, thunderer, (into the pitchers), as cows go to their stalls : come, Indra, the first of those who are to be worshipped.

#### Ṛk 2

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> या ते॑ का॒कुत्सुकृ॑ता॒ या वरि॑ष्ठा॒ यया॒ शश्व॒त्पिब॑सि॒ मध्व॑  ॒
> ऊर्मिं ।  
> तया॑ पाहि॒ प्र ते॑ अध्व॒र्युर॑स्था॒त्सं ते॒ वज्रो॑ वर्ततामिंद्र  ॒
> गव्युः ॥ २ ॥

### Printed p. 88

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> या । ते । काकुत् । सुकृता । या । वरिष्ठा । यया । शश्वत् । पिबसि । मध्वः ।  
> ऊर्मिं ।  
> शया । पाहि । प्र । ते । अध्वर्युः । अस्थात् । सं । ते । वज्रः । वर्ततां । इंद्र ।  
> गव्युः ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे इंद्र या ते तव काकुज्जिह्वा सुकृता सुष्टु धात्रा निर्मिता । या च
> वरिष्टोरुतमा विस्तीर्णतमा । यया च मध्वो मधुनः सोमस्योर्मिं रसं
> शश्वद्बहुकृत्वः पिबसि तया जिह्वयास्मदीयं सोमं पाहि । पिब । तदर्थमध्वर्युरध्वरस्य नेता ऋत्विगृहीतसोमः सन् प्रास्थात् । हविर्धानादुत्तरवेदिदेशं
> प्रत्यगमत् । अपि च हे इंद्र गव्युः शत्रुसंबंधिनीर्गा आत्मन इच्छन् ते त्वदीयो
> वज्रः सं वर्ततां । शत्रुभिः संगतो भवतु । तान् हंतित्यर्थः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Indra, that tongue of yours which has been well fashioned by the creator, which is the most extensive, and with which you drink again and again the essence of the sweet Soma, with that tongue drink our Soma. For that purpose the Adhvaryu, the leader of the rite, having taken the Soma by the hands of the priests, has set out from the place of the oblations to the region of the northern altar. Moreover, O Indra, desiring for yourself the cows belonging to the enemies, let your thunderbolt come together with the enemies; that is, let it kill them.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- ते (*te*)—your
- या (*yā*)—which
- काकुत् (*kākut*)—tongue
- सुकृता (*sukṛtā*)—has been made most excellently
- या (*yā*)—which
- वरिष्ठा (*variṣṭhā*)—is most extensive
- यया (*yayā*)—with which tongue
- मध्वः (*madhvaḥ*)—of the sweet Soma
- ऊर्मिं (*ūrmiṃ*)—the juice
- शश्वत् (*śaśvat*)—many times
- पिबसि (*pibasi*)—you drink
- तया (*tayā*)—with that same tongue
- पाहि (*pāhi*)—drink this Soma juice
- अध्वर्युः (*adhvaryuḥ*)—the Adhvaryu
- ते प्र अस्टात् (*te pra asṭāt*)—stands ready to offer it to you
- इंद्र (*iṃdra*)—O Indra
- गव्युः (*gavyuḥ*)—desirous of obtaining cows
- ते (*te*)—your
- वज्रः (*vajraḥ*)—thunderbolt weapon
- सं वर्ततां (*saṃ vartatāṃ*)—let it be hurled against the enemies

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, with that tongue of yours which has been made most excellently and is most extensive, with which you drink the sweet Soma juice many times, drink this Soma juice with that same tongue. The Adhvaryu stands ready to offer it to you. O Indra, let your thunderbolt weapon, desirous of obtaining cows, be hurled against the enemies.

### Printed p. 89

##### English translation
Drink, Indra. with that well-formed and expanded tongue wherewith you ever quaff the juice of the sweet (Soma): before you stands the ministrant priest: let your bolt. Indra. designed (to recover) the cattle, be hurled (against your foes).

#### Ṛk 3

**NOTE**

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> ए॒ष द्र॒प्सो वृ॑ष॒भो वि॒श्वरू॑प॒ इंद्रा॑य॒ वृष्णे॒ सम॑कारि॒  ॑
> सोमः ।  
> ए॒तं पि॑ब हरिवः स्थातरुग्र॒ यस्येशि॑षे प्र॒दिवि॒ यस्ते॒ अन्नं॑ ॥२॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> एषः । द्रप्सः । वृषभः । विश्वरूपः । इंद्राय । वृष्णे । सं । आकारि । सोमः ।  
> एतं । पिब । हरिवः । स्थातः । उग्र । यस्य । ईशिषे । प्रदिवि । यः । ते । अन्नं ॥२॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> द्रप्पो द्रवणशीलो वृषभः कामानां वर्षिता विश्वरूपो बहुविधरूपोपेत
> ईदृग्विध एष सोमो वृष्णे कामानां वर्षित्र इंद्रायेंद्रस्य पानार्थं समकारि ।
> अभिषवादिभिः संस्कृतोऽभूत् । हे हरिवः । हरी अश्वे । तद्वन् हे स्थातः सर्वेषामधिष्ठातरुग्रोद्दूर्णबलेंद्र एतमीदृशं सोमं पिब । यस्य सोमस्य प्रदिवि ॥
> जातावेकवचनं ॥ प्रभूतेषु दिवसेषु प्रगतेश्वहःसु वेशिषे त्वमेवेश्वरो भवसि ।
> यश्च स्तोमस्ते तवान्न मन्नत्वेन परिकल्पित एतं पिबेत्यन्वयः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

This Soma, flowing, showering desires, possessed of forms of many kinds, has been prepared (समकारि (*samakāri*)) for the drinking of Indra, the showerer of desires; it has been refined by pressing and the like. O Indra, lord of the bay horses, stander, presider over all, of mighty strength, drink this Soma, over which you alone are the lord throughout many past days, and which is conceived as your food. The construction is: drink this.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- द्रप्पः (*drappaḥ*)—flowing by nature
- वृषभः (*vṛṣabhaḥ*)—showering desires
- विश्वरूपः (*viśvarūpaḥ*)—possessed of forms of many kinds
- एषः सोमः (*eṣaḥ somaḥ*)—this Soma juice
- वृष्णे (*vṛṣṇe*)—showerer of desires
- इंद्राय (*iṃdrāya*)—for the drinking of Indra
- समकारि (*samakāri*)—has been well refined and made ready
- हरिवः (*harivaḥ*)—O you who have horses
- स्थातः (*sthātaḥ*)—who stand firm as the foremost of all
- उग्र (*ugra*)—O Indra, of terrible strength
- यस्य (*yasya*)—of which Soma
- प्रदिवि (*pradivi*)—for many days
- ईशिषे (*īśiṣe*)—you are the lord

### Printed p. 90

- यः (*yaḥ*)—which Soma
- ते (*te*)—for you
- अन्नं (*annaṃ*)—is of the nature of food
- एकं (*ekaṃ*)—this such Soma
- पिब (*piba*)—drink

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

This Soma juice, flowing by nature, showering desires, possessed of forms of many kinds, has been well refined and made ready for the drinking of Indra. O Indra, who have horses and stand as the foremost of all, you who are the lord of the Soma juices for many days, drink this Soma, which is of the nature of food for you.

##### English translation
This undropping, omniform Soma, the showerer (of benefits , has been duly prepared for Indra, the showerer (of rain): lord of steeds, ruler over all, mighty (Indra), drink this over which you have of old presided, which is your food.

#### Ṛk 4

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> सु॒तः सोमो॒ असु॑तांद्र॒ वस्या॑न॒यं श्रेया॑ञ्चिकि॒तुषे॒  ॑
> रणाय ।  
> ए॒तं ति॑तिर्व॒ उप॑ याहि य॒ज्ञं तेन॒ विश्वा॒स्तवि॑षी॒रा पृ॑णस्व ॥४॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> सुतः । सोमः । असुतात् । इंद्र । वस्यान् । अयं । श्रेयान् । चिकितुषे । रणाय।  
> एतं । तितिर्वः । उप । याहि । यज्ञं । तेन । विश्वाः । तविषीः । आ । पृणस्व ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे इंद्र सुतोऽभिषुतोऽयं सोमोऽसुतादकृताभिषवात्सोमादन्यदीयाद्वस्यान् वसीयान् वसुमत्तरः प्रशस्यतरः । तथा चिकितुषे विदुषेऽभिज्ञाय
> तुभ्यं रणाय रमणीयाय तदर्थं श्रेयान् श्रेष्ठश्च भवति । त्वामतिशयेन
> रमयितेत्यर्थः । हे तितिर्वः शत्रूणां तरितरिंद्र यजनसाधनमेतं सोममुप
> याहि । उपागच्छ । तेन च सोमेन विश्वास्तविषीः सर्वाणि बलान्या पृणस्व । आपूरय ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Indra, this Soma that has been pressed is better, richer and more praiseworthy than any unpressed Soma of another. Likewise, for you who are knowing and discerning, it is the best and the most delightful: it will give you the greatest delight. O Titirva, Indra, overcomer of the enemies, come to this Soma, the means of sacrifice; come near. And with that Soma fill up all your powers.

### Printed p. 91

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- इंद्र (*iṃdra*)—O Indra
- सुतः (*sutaḥ*)—which has been pressed
- अयं सोमः (*ayaṃ somaḥ*)—this Soma
- असुतात् (*asutāt*)—than the Soma that has not been pressed
- वस्यान् (*vasyān*)—is better
- चिकितुषे (*cikituṣe*)—to you who are fully wise
- रणाय (*raṇāya*)—to cause delight
- श्रेयान् (*śreyān*)—is better
- तितिर्वः (*titirvaḥ*)—O Indra, who cast down the enemies
- यज्ञं (*yajñaṃ*)—which is the means of sacrifice
- एतं (*etaṃ*)—to this Soma
- उप याहि (*upa yāhi*)—be gracious and come
- तेन (*tena*)—with this same Soma
- विश्वाः तविषीः (*viśvāḥ taviṣīḥ*)—all your powers
- आ पृणस्व (*ā pṛṇasva*)—fill up

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, this Soma that has been pressed and made ready is better than the Soma that has not been pressed. It is better for causing delight to you who are fully wise. O Indra, who cast the enemies down, be gracious and come to the place where this Soma, the means of sacrifice, is. With this same Soma fill up all your powers.

##### English translation
The effuscd Soma, Indra, is more excellent than that which is not effused : it is better (qualified) to give pleasure to you, who are capable of judging : overcomer (of enemies), approach this sacrifice, and thereby perfect all yours powers.

> मुखः सन् आ याहि । आगच्छ । अस्मदीयः सोमश्च ते तन्ने तव शरीरायारं

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Indra, we invoke you; therefore, facing us, come. And our Soma, for your body,

### Printed p. 92

> पर्याप्तं भवाति । भवतु । त्वदीयं शरीरं यथाभिलाषं तर्पयत्वित्यर्थः । हे
> शतक्रतो बहुविधकर्मन्निंद्र त्वं च सुतेष्वभिषुतेष्वस्मदीयेषु सोमेषु
> मादयस्व । तृप्स्य ॥ मद तृप्तियोगे ॥ तदनंतरं पृतनासु संग्रामेष्वस्मान्प्राव ।
> प्रकर्षेण रक्ष । न केवलं पृतनासु किंतु विक्षु सर्वासु प्रजास्वस्मान्प्राव । प्ररक्ष ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

may be sufficient; that is, let it satisfy your body as it desires. And you, O Śatakratu, Indra, doer of many kinds of deeds, delight yourself in our Soma that has been pressed; be satisfied. Thereafter protect us well in the battles. Not only in the battles, but protect us among all the peoples.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- इंद्र (*iṃdra*)—O Indra
- त्वा (*tvā*)—you
- ह्वयामसि (*hvayāmasi*)—we invoke with prayer
- आर्वाज् (*ārvāj*)—facing us
- आ याहि (*ā yāhi*)—be gracious and come
- सोमः (*somaḥ*)—this Soma
- ते (*te*)—your
- तन्ने (*tanne*)—for the nourishment of the body
- आरं (*āraṃ*)—as sufficient
- भवाति (*bhavāti*)—let it be
- शतक्रतो (*śatakrato*)—O Indra, doer of many deeds
- सुतेषु (*suteṣu*)—in these Somas that have been pressed
- मादयस्व (*mādayasva*)—find satisfaction
- पृतनासु (*pṛtanāsu*)—in the battles
- आस्मान् (*āsmān*)—us
- प्र अव (*pra ava*)—protect completely
- विक्षु (*vikṣu*)—among all the peoples
- प्र (*pra*)—protect us

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, we invoke you with prayer. Be gracious and come facing us. Let this Soma be sufficient for the nourishment of your body. O Indra, doer of many deeds, find satisfaction in these Somas that have been pressed and made ready. Protect us completely in the battles. Protect us also among all the peoples.

##### English translation
We invoke you, Indra, come down : may the Soma be sufficient for (the satisfaction of) your person: exalt Shatakaratu with the libations, defend us in combats, and against the people.

## Sūkta 42 (Maṇḍala 6)

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> प्रत्यस्मा इति चतुर्ऋचमेकोनविंशं सूक्तं भरद्वाजस्यार्षमैंद्रं ।
> चतुर्थी बृहती शिष्टा अनुष्टुभः । तथा चानुक्रांतं । प्रत्यस्मै चतुष्कमानुष्टुभं
> बृहत्यंतमिति ॥ प्रातःसवनेच्छावाकस्येदमुन्नीयमानसूक्तं । सूत्रितं च । प्रत्यस्मा इत्युन्नीयमानायानूच्य । आ. ५-७ । इति.

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The sūkta beginning प्रत्यस्मा इति (*pratyasmā iti*) has four Ṛks and is the nineteenth sūkta. Its seer is Bharadvāja (Ārṣa) and its deity is Indra. The fourth Ṛk is a Bṛhatī and the remaining ones are Anuṣṭubh. It is listed in the Anukramaṇī thus: प्रत्यस्मै चतुष्कमानुष्टुभं बृहत्यंतमिति (*pratyasmai catuṣkamānuṣṭubhaṃ bṛhatyaṃtamiti*). At the morning pressing this is the unnīyamāna sūkta of the Acchāvāka. It is stated in the sūtra: प्रत्यस्मा इत्युन्नीयमानायानूच्य (*pratyasmā ityunnīyamānāyānūcya*) (Ā. Sū. 5-7).

### Printed p. 93

##### Anuvāda

This sūkta beginning "Pratyasmai" is the nineteenth sūkta in the third anuvāka. It has four Ṛks. Bharadvāja is the seer of this sūkta and Indra is the deity. The fourth Ṛk of this sūkta is in the Bṛhatī metre; the remaining Ṛks are in the Anuṣṭup metre. In the Anukramaṇikā it is said: प्रत्यस्मै चतुष्कमानुष्टुभं बृहत्यंतं (*pratyasmai catuṣkamānuṣṭubhaṃ bṛhatyaṃtaṃ*). The use of this sūkta is for the unnīyamāna sūkta to be recited by the priest called Acchāvāka at the time of the morning pressing, as is explained by the sūtra of the Āśvalāyana Śrauta Sūtra, प्रत्यस्मा इत्युन्नीयमानायानूच्य (*pratyasmā ityunnīyamānāyānūcya*) (Ā. 5-7).

##### Particulars

- Sūkta 42
- Maṇḍala 6; Anuvāka 3; Sūkta 42
- Aṣṭaka 4; Adhyāya 7; Varga 14
- Number of Ṛks in the sūkta: 4
- Ṛṣi: Bharadvāja Bārhaspatya
- Devatā: Indra
- Chandas: Ṛks 1-3, Anuṣṭup; Ṛk 4, Bṛhatī

#### Ṛk 1

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> प्रत्य॑स्मै॒ पिपी॑षते॒ विश्वा॑नि वि॒दुषे॑ भर ।  
> अ॒रं॒ग॒माय॒ जग्म॒येऽप॑श्चाद्दघ्वने॒ नरे॑ ॥ १ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> प्रति । अस्मै । पिपीषते । विश्वानि । विदुषे । भर ।  
> आरंगमाय । जग्मये । अपश्चत् दघ्वने । नरे ॥ १ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे अध्वर्यो अस्मा इंद्राय प्रति भर । अभिहर । सोमं प्रयच्छेत्यर्थः ।
> कीदृशायेंद्राय । पिपीषते पातुमिच्छते विश्वानि सर्वाणि वेद्यानि विदुषे जानतेऽरंगमायॆ पर्याप्तगमनाय जग्मये यज्ञेषु गमनशीलायापश्चाद्दघ्वने ॥
> दघिर्गतिकर्मा ॥ अपश्चाद्गमनाय सर्वेषामग्रगामिने नरे नेत्रे यज्ञानां ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Adhvaryu, bring forward to this Indra; that is, offer him the Soma. To what kind of Indra? To him who wishes to drink; to him who knows all things that are to be known; to him whose going is sufficient, who goes readily to the sacrifices, who goes in front of all and never behind, the leader of the sacrifices.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- पिपीषते (*pipīṣate*)—who wishes to drink
- विश्वानि (*viśvāni*)—all that is to be known
- विदुषे (*viduṣe*)—who knows
- अरंगमाय (*araṃgamāya*)—whose going is complete
- जग्मये (*jagmaye*)—who goes readily to the sacrifices

### Printed p. 94

- अपश्चाद्दघ्वने (*apaścāddaghvane*)—who goes in front of all
- नरे (*nare*)—who is the leader
- अस्मै (*asmai*)—directed to this Indra
- प्रतिभर (*pratibhara*)—offer the Soma

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Adhvaryu, offer the Soma to this Indra, who wishes to drink, who knows all that is to be known, whose going is complete, who goes readily to the sacrifices, who goes in front of all, and who is the leader.

##### English translation
Offer, (priests), the libation to him who is desirous to drink ; who knows all things; whose movements are all-sufficient; who goes readily (to sacrifices); the leader (of holy rites), following no one.

#### Ṛk 2

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> एमे॑नं प्र॒त्येत॑न॒ सोमे॑भिः सोम॒पात॑मं ।  
> अम॑त्रेभिर्ऋजी॒षिण॒मिंद्रं॑ सु॒तेभि॒रिंदु॑भिः ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> आं । ईं । एनं । प्रतिएतन । सोमेभिः । सोमपातमं ।  
> अमत्रेभिः । यजीषिणं । इंद्रं । सुतेभिः । इंदुभिः ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> तृतीये स्वरसामि निष्केवल्य एमेनमिति स्तोत्रियस्तृचः । सूत्रितं च ।
> एमेनं प्रत्येतनेत्याद्यो ना सर्वेषां । आ. ८-५ । इति ॥
> हे अध्वर्यवः सोमेभिः सोमैः करणभूतैः सोमपातममतिशयेन
> सोमस्य पातारमेनमिंद्रमा अभिमुखं प्रत्येतन । प्रतिगच्छत । ईमिति निपातोऽन
> र्थकः । कीदृशमींद्रं । अमत्रेभिरमत्रैः सोमपात्रैर्ग्रहचमसादिभिर्युजीषिणं ।
> ऋजीषं शत्रूणामुपार्जकं बलं । तद्वंतं । यद्वा । ऋजीषिणमित्युत्तरत
> संबंधनीयं । सुतेभिरभिषुतैरिंदुभिः सोमैर्युजीषिणं । गतसारः सोम
> ऋजीषः । तद्वंतं । अथवामत्रेभिरमत्रैरमात्रैरपरिमितैरभिषुतैः सोमैर्युजीषिणं ॥ ऋजेर्गत्यर्थाद्भावसाधन ऋजीष शब्दः । ततो मत्वर्थीय इनिः ॥ संगतमित्यर्थः । एवंविधमिंद्रं प्रतिगच्छतेत्यन्वयः । अन्य आह । अमत्रेभिर्ग्रहचमसादिगतैरभिषुतैः सोमैर्युजीषिणं बलवंतमेनमिंद्रं प्रतिगच्छतेति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

In the third (round), in the Svarasāman Niṣkevalya, the triplet beginning एमेनमिति (*emenamiti*) is the stotriya.**NOTE** It is stated in the sūtra: एमेनं प्रत्येतनेत्याद्यो ना सर्वेषां (*emenaṃ pratyetanetyādyo nā sarveṣāṃ*) (Ā. Sū. 8-5). O Adhvaryus, go towards this Indra, who is the greatest drinker of Soma, with the Somas as the means. The particle ईं (*īṃ*) has no meaning. What kind of Indra? One who is possessed of ṛjīṣa with the Soma vessels (अमत्रेभिः (*amatrebhiḥ*)), the cups, ladles and the like. Ṛjīṣa is the strength that gathers from the enemies; he possesses it.**NOTE** Or else, ṛjīṣiṇam is to be connected with what follows: he is possessed of ṛjīṣa with the pressed Somas (इंदुभिः (*iṃdubhiḥ*)); Soma whose essence is gone is ṛjīṣa, and he possesses it. Or else, अमत्रेभिः (*amatrebhiḥ*) means with immeasurable pressed Somas; he is connected with them, that is, united. The construction is: go to an Indra of this kind. Another says: go to this mighty Indra with the Somas that have been pressed and put into the cups, the ladles and the like.

### Printed p. 95

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- (O Adhvaryus) सोमेभिः (*somebhiḥ*)—with the Soma juices
- सोमपातमं (*somapātamaṃ*)—who drinks Soma to the utmost
- एनं (*enaṃ*)—Indra
- आ प्रत्येतन (*ā pratyetana*)—go forth to meet and welcome
- ऋजीषिणं (*ṛjīṣiṇaṃ*)—who has the strength that destroys enemies
- इंद्रं (*iṃdraṃ*)—Indra
- सुतेभिः (*sutebhiḥ*)—that have been pressed
- अमत्रेभिः (*amatrebhiḥ*)—that are in the camasa vessel
- इंदुभिः (*iṃdubhiḥ*)—with the Soma juices (meet him)

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Adhvaryus, go forth to meet Indra, who drinks Soma to the utmost, together with the Soma juices. With the Soma juices that have been pressed and that stand in the camasa vessel, meet Indra, who has the strength that destroys enemies.

##### English translation
Proceed to the presence of that deep quaffer of the Soma, with the
Soma juices; to the vigorous Indra with vessels (filled) with the effused
libations.

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

In the Iṣṭi called the third Svara-sāman, in the Niṣkevalya-śastra mantras, the tṛca beginning एमेनं (*emenaṃ*) is to be recited as the Stotriya tṛca; this is explained by the sūtra of the Āśvalāyana Śrauta Sūtra, एमेनं प्रत्येतनेत्याद्यो वा सर्वॆषां (*emenaṃ pratyetanetyādyo vā sarvèṣāṃ*) (Ā. Sū. 8-5).

#### Ṛk 3

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> यदी॑ सु॒तेभि॒रिंदु॑भिः॒ सोमे॑भिः प्रति॒भूष॑थ ।  
> वेदा॒ विश्व॑स्य॒ मेधि॑रो धृ॒षत्तंत॒मिदेष॑ते ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> यदि । सुतेभिः । इंदुभिः । सोमेभिः । प्रतिभूषथ ।  
> वेद । विश्वस्य । मेधिरः । धृषत् । तंतं । इत् । आ । ईषते ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे अध्वर्यवः सुतेभिरभिषुतैरिंदुभिरुंदनशीलैर्दीर्प्तैर्वा सोमेभिः
> सोमैर्यदि प्रतिभूषथ इंद्रं प्रति यूयं प्रतिगच्छथ॥ भू प्राप्तावित्यस्यॆतद्रूपं॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Sāyaṇa addresses the Adhvaryus: if you go towards Indra with the Somas that are pressed and इंदुभिः (*iṃdubhiḥ*), that is, flowing or shining, then the sense of प्रतिभूषथ (*pratibhūṣatha*) is that you approach Indra; भू (*bhū*) is used in the sense of obtaining.

### Printed p. 96

> शदानीं मेधिरो मेधावी । मेधो यज्ञस्तद्वान्वा । स इंद्रो विश्वस्य विश्वं
> सर्वं भवदीयं कामं वेद । वेत्ति । जानाति । ज्ञात्वा च धृषत् शत्रूणां धर्षकः
> सन् तं तमित्तं तं काममेवैषते । प्रापयति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Then Indra, the मेधिरः (*medhiraḥ*), is wise; or मेधो (*medho*) is the sacrifice and he is its possessor. That Indra knows all your desire; knowing it, and being the assailant of enemies, he brings about each and every desire, that is, he makes you obtain it.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- (O Adhvaryus) सुतेभिः (*sutebhiḥ*)—that are pressed
- इंदुभिः (*iṃdubhiḥ*)—that flow
- सोमेभिः (*somebhiḥ*)—with the Soma juices
- यदि (*yadi*)—when
- प्रति भूषथ (*prati bhūṣatha*)—you go to meet him (then)
- मेधिरः (*medhiraḥ*)—Indra, who is wise
- विश्वस्य (*viśvasya*)—all your desires
- वेद (*veda*)—he knows (and)
- धृषत् (*dhṛṣat*)—being the destroyer of enemies
- तंतमित् (*taṃtamit*)—all those desires
- आ ईषते (*ā īṣate*)—he makes you obtain

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Adhvaryus, when you go to meet Indra with the pressed and flowing Soma juices, then the wise Indra knows all your desires. And, being the destroyer of enemies, he makes you obtain all your desires.

##### English translation
When, with the effused and flowing Soma juices, you come into his presence, the sagacious (Indra) knows your wish, and the suppressor (of enemies) assuredly grants it, whatever it may be.

#### Ṛk 4

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अ॒स्मा अ॑स्मा॒ इदंध॒सोऽध्व॑यो॒ प्र भ॑रा सु॒तं ।  
> कु॒वित्स॑मस्य॒ जेन्य॑स्य॒ शर्ध॑तो॒ऽभिश॑स्तेरव॒स्पर॑त् ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अस्मै अस्मै । इत् । अंधसः । अध्वयो इति । प्र । भर । सुतं ।  
> कुवित् । समस्य । जेन्यस्य । शर्धतः । अभिशस्तेः । अव्यस्परत् ॥ ४ ॥

### Printed p. 97

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> अस्मा अस्मा इत् आस्मा एवेंद्राय नान्यस्य हे अध्वर्यो त्वमंधसः
> सोमलक्षणस्यान्नस्य सुतमभिषुतं रसं प्र भर । संप्रहर । प्रयच्छेति यावत् ।
> स चेंद्रः समस्य सर्वस्य जेन्यस्य जेतव्यस्य शर्धत उत्सहमानस्य शत्रोरभिशस्तेरभिशंसनात् तत्कृताद्धिंसनात्कुविद्बहुशोऽवस्परत् अस्मान्पालयतु ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

अस्मा अस्मा इत् (*asmā asmā it*) means: to this Indra alone, not to another. O Adhvaryu, offer, that is, bestow, the pressed juice of the food called Soma. And may that Indra protect us again and again from the harm done by every enemy who is to be conquered and who is eager and violent.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अध्वर्यो (*adhvaryo*)—O Adhvaryu
- अंधसः (*aṃdhasaḥ*)—of the food in the form of Soma
- सुतं (*sutaṃ*)—the pressed juice
- अस्माअस्मा इत् (*asmāasmā it*)—to Indra alone, and to no other deity
- प्र भर (*pra bhara*)—offer in the best manner (may this same Indra)
- समस्य (*samasya*)—all
- जेन्यस्य (*jenyasya*)—who is to be conquered
- शर्धतः (*śardhataḥ*)—and who is violent; the enemy
- अभिशस्तेः (*abhiśasteḥ*)—from the destruction
- कुवित् (*kuvit*)—many times
- अवस्परत् (*avasparat*)—protect us

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Adhvaryu, offer in the best manner the pressed juice in the form of Soma to Indra alone, and not to any other deity. May this same Indra protect us many times by destroying every enemy who is to be conquered and who is violent.

##### English translation
Offer, priest, to him, and him (only) this libation of (sacrificial)
food, and may he ever defend us against the malignity of every superable
adversary.

## Sūkta 43 (Maṇḍala 6)
The forty-third Sūkta.

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> यस्य त्यदिति चतुर्यचं विंशं सूक्तं भरद्वाजस्यार्षमौष्ठिहमैंद्रं ।
> अनुक्रम्यते च । यसौष्ठिहमिति ॥ महाव्रतेऽपि निष्केवल्येऽप्यौष्ठिहतृचाशीतावेत
> तूक्तं । तथैव पंचमारण्यके सूत्रितं । यस्य त्यच्छंबरं मद इति त्रयस्तृजा
> गायत्र्यः । ऐ. आ. ५-२-५ । इति ॥
> XXI 7

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The Sūkta यस्य त्यदिति (*yasya tyaditi*) has four Ṛks and is the twentieth Sūkta. Its seer is Bharadvāja, its deity is Indra, and its metre is Uṣṇih. The Anukramaṇī says यस्यौष्ठिहं (*yasyauṣṭhihaṃ*). In the Mahāvrata and in the Niṣkevalya also, this Sūkta is employed for the Uṣṇih tṛca. Likewise it is laid down in the fifth Āraṇyaka: यस्य त्यच्छंबरं मद इति त्रयस्तृजा गायत्र्यः (*yasya tyacchaṃbaraṃ mada iti trayastṛjā gāyatryaḥ*) (Ai. Ā. 5-2-5).

### Printed p. 98

Anuvāda: This Sūkta beginning यस्य त्यत् (*yasya tyat*) is the twentieth Sūkta in the third Anuvāka; it has four Ṛks. Bharadvāja is the seer of this Sūkta, Indra is the deity, and Uṣṇik is the metre. In the Anukramaṇī it is said यस्यौष्ठिहं (*yasyauṣṭhihaṃ*). It is indicated by the sentence यस्य त्यच्छंबरं मद इति त्रयस्तृचा गायत्र्यः (*yasya tyacchaṃbaraṃ mada iti trayastṛcā gāyatryaḥ*) (Ai. Ā. 5-2-5) in the Pañcamāraṇyaka of the Aitareya Āraṇyaka that, when the Niṣkevalya-śastra mantras are recited in the Mahāvrata Iṣṭi, this Sūkta is employed for the Ṛks of the Uṣṇik metre.

Sūkta—43
Maṇḍala—6. Anuvāka—3. Sūkta—43.
Aṣṭaka—4. Adhyāya—7. Varga—15.
Number of Ṛks in the Sūkta—4.
Ṛṣi—भरद्वाजो बार्दस्पत्यः (*bharadvājo bārdaspatyaḥ*).
Devatā—इंद्रः (*iṃdraḥ*).
Chandas—उष्णिक् (*uṣṇik*).

#### Ṛk 1

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> यस्य॒ त्यच्छंब॑रं॒ मदे॒ दिवो॑दासाय रं॒धयः॑ ।  
> अ॒यं स सोम॑ इंद्र ते सु॒तः पिब॑ ॥ १ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> यस्य । त्यत् । शंबरं । मदे । दिवःऽदासाय । रंधयः ।  
> अयं । सः । सोमः । इंद्र । ते । सुतः । पिब ॥ १ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे इंद्र यस्य सोमस्य मदे पानेन जनिते हर्षे सति शंबरमसुरं
> दिवोदासाय राजे रंधयः अहिंसीः । यद्वा । वशीकृतवानसि ॥ रध्यतिर्वशगमने ।
> नि. ६-३२ । इति यास्कः ॥ त्यदिति क्रियाविशेषणं । त्यत्तत्प्रसिद्धं यथा भवति तथारंधय
> इत्यर्थः । हे इंद्र सोऽयं सोमस्ते त्वदर्थं सुतोऽभिषुतः । आतस्तं पिब ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Indra, in the exhilaration produced by drinking which Soma you destroyed the asura Śambara for King Divodāsa, or, as he also says, you subdued him; Yāska says रध्यतिर्वशगमने (*radhyatirvaśagamane*) (Ni. 6-32). त्यत् (*tyat*) is an adverb of the action: you destroyed in that well-known manner. O Indra, this very Soma has been pressed for you; therefore drink it.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- इंद्र (*iṃdra*)—O Indra
- यस्य मदे (*yasya made*)—satisfied by drinking which Soma juice
- दिवोदासाय (*divodāsāya*)—for the king named Divodāsa
- शंबरं (*śaṃbaraṃ*)—Śambara, the asura
- रंधयः (*raṃdhayaḥ*)—you destroyed

### Printed p. 99

- त्यत् (*tyat*)—well-known
- सः आयं सोमः (*saḥ āyaṃ somaḥ*)—that same Soma juice
- ते (*te*)—for you
- सुतः (*sutaḥ*)—has been pressed
- पिब (*piba*)—drink it

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, satisfied by drinking which Soma juice you destroyed the asura Śambara for the king named Divodāsa, that same well-known Soma juice has been pressed for you. Drink it.

##### English translation
This Soma, in the exhilaration of which it is known that you have subdued Sambara for (the sake of) Divodasa, is poured out, Indra, for you; drink.

#### Ṛk 2

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> यस्य॑ तीव्र॒सुतं॒ मदं॒ मध्य॒मंतं॑ च॒ रक्ष॑से ।  
> अ॒यं स सोम॑ इंद्र ते सु॒तः पिब॑ ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> यस्य । तीव्रसुतं । मदं । मध्यं । अंतं । च । रक्षसे ।  
> अयं । सः । सोमः । इंद्र । ते । सुतः । पिब ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे इंद्र त्वं यस्य सोमस्यावयवभूतं तीव्रसुतं । तीक्ष्णं सुतमभिषवो
> यस्य स तीव्रसुतः प्रातःसवनिकः । तं मदं मदकरं सोमरसं मध्यं मध्यंदिनसवनगतमंतं तृतीयसवनगतं च सोमरसं रक्षसे रक्षसि सोऽयं सोमस्त्वदर्थमभिषुतस्तं पिबेति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Indra, you protect the portion of which Soma, the तीव्रसुतं (*tīvrasutaṃ*), that is, the one whose pressing is sharp, belonging to the morning pressing; that intoxicating Soma juice; the मध्यं (*madhyaṃ*), belonging to the midday pressing; and the अंतं (*aṃtaṃ*), belonging to the third pressing. This Soma has been pressed for you; drink it.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- इंद्र (*iṃdra*)—O Indra
- यस्य (*yasya*)—in the portion of which Soma juice
- तीव्रसुतं (*tīvrasutaṃ*)—pressed at the morning pressing and strong
- मदं (*madaṃ*)—which is intoxicating
- मध्यं (*madhyaṃ*)—pressed at the midday pressing
- अंतं (*aṃtaṃ*)—pressed at the third pressing; which Soma juices
- रक्षसे (*rakṣase*)—you protect with regard
- स अयं सोमः (*sa ayaṃ somaḥ*)—that same Soma
- ते सुतः (*te sutaḥ*)—has been pressed for you
- पिब (*piba*)—drink it

### Printed p. 100

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, drink all the several Soma juices there are: the intoxicating one, the one pressed and made ready at the morning pressing, the one pressed at the midday pressing, and the one pressed at the third pressing.

##### English translation
This Soma, the exhilarating draught of which, when fresh effused (at dawn), or at noon, or at the last (or evening worship), you cherish is poured out, Indra, for you: drink.

#### Ṛk 3

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> यस्य॒ गा अं॒तरश्म॑नो॒ मदे॑ दृ॒ळ्हा अ॒वासृ॑जः ।  
> अ॒यं स सोम॑ इंद्र ते सु॒तः पिब॑ ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> यस्य । गाः । अंतः । अश्मनः । मदे । दृळ्हाः । अव्यअसृजः ।  
> आयं । सः । सोमः । इंद्र । ते । सुतः । पिब ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> यस्य सोमस्य मदे मदकरे रसे पीते सत्यश्मनो वलासुरेण विहितस्य
> पर्वतस्यांतर्मध्यॆ विद्यमाना दृळ्हा दृढसिधानाश्च गाः पणिभिरपहृता अवास्पृजः
> वलस्य बंधनाद्वस्यृजः स सोम इत्यादि गतं ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

When the Soma, the intoxicating juice, has been drunk, you released the cows that had been carried off by the Paṇis, which lay inside the mountain made by the asura Vala, firm and tightly shut in. The rest, स सोम इत्यादि गतं (*sa soma ityādi gataṃ*), has been explained already.**NOTE**

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- इंद्र (*iṃdra*)—O Indra
- यस्य (*yasya*)—by drinking which Soma
- मदे (*made*)—when satisfaction arose
- अश्मनः (*aśmanaḥ*)—of the mountain
- अंतः (*aṃtaḥ*)—in the middle
- दृळ्ताः (*dṛḷtāḥ*)—though firmly bound
- गाः (*gāḥ*)—the cows
- अवासृजः (*avāsṛjaḥ*)—you released
- अयं स सोमः (*ayaṃ sa somaḥ*)—that same Soma
- ते (*te*)—for you
- सुतः (*sutaḥ*)—has been pressed
- पिब (*piba*)—drink it

### Printed p. 101

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, by the satisfaction of which Soma-drinking you released the cows although they were firmly bound in the middle of the mountain, that same Soma has been pressed for you. Drink it.

##### English translation
This Soma, in the exhilaration of which you have liberated the cattle, firm (fastened) within the rock, is poured out, Indra, for you: drink.

#### Ṛk 4

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> यस्य॑ मंदा॒नो अंध॑सो॒ माघो॑नं दधि॒षे शवः॑ ।  
> अ॒यं स सोम॑ इंद्र ते सु॒तः पिब॑ ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> यस्य । मंदानः । अंधसः । माघोनं । दधिषे । शवः ।  
> अयं । सः । सोमः । इंद्र । ते । सुतः । पिब ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे इंद्र त्वं यस्यांधसः सोमलक्षणस्यान्नस्य पानेन मंदानो मंदमानो
> हृष्टः सन् माघोनमैंद्रं शवोऽसाधारणं बलं दधिषे धारयसि । येन
> सोमेन तवेंद्रत्वं जातमित्यर्थः । सोऽयं सोमः सुतोऽभिषुतस्तं पिब ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Indra, you are gladdened by drinking the Soma-food, and so you bear the strength शवः (*śavaḥ*) that belongs to Maghavan, Indra's own and uncommon might. By which Soma your Indra-hood came about. That same Soma has been pressed; drink it.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- इंद्र (*iṃdra*)—O Indra
- यस्य (*yasya*)—which
- अंधस्य (*aṃdhasya*)—by the drinking of the Soma juice
- मंदानः (*maṃdānaḥ*)—becoming glad
- माघोनं (*māghonaṃ*)—the lordship over all called "Maghavā"
- शवः (*śavaḥ*)—and the uncommon strength
- दधिषे (*dadhiṣe*)—you possess
- सः आयं सोमः (*saḥ āyaṃ somaḥ*)—that same Soma
- ते (*te*)—for you
- सुतः (*sutaḥ*)—has been pressed
- पिब (*piba*)—drink it

### Printed p. 102

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, by drinking which Soma juice you became glad and obtained the lordship called "Maghavā" and possess uncommon strength, that same Soma has been pressed for you. Drink it.

##### English translation
This Soma, exhilarated (by drinking) of which (sacrificial) food you possess the might of Maghavan, is poured out, Indra, for you : drink.

## Sūkta 44 (Maṇḍala 6)
The forty-fourth Sūkta.

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> चतुर्थेऽनुवाके षट् सूक्तानि । तत्र यो रयिव इति चतुर्विंशत्यैचं
> प्रथमं सूक्तं बृहस्पतिपुत्रस्य शंयूरार्षमैंद्रं । आदितः षडनुष्टुभः सप्तम्यष्टमीनवम्यो विराजोऽष्टम्येकैव वा विराट् शिष्टास्त्रिष्टुभः । तथा चानुक्रांतं ।
> यो रयिवश्चतुर्विंशतिः शंयुर्बार्हस्पत्यो ह्यादौ षळनुष्टुभस्तिस्रश्च विराजो
> मध्यमैव वासामिति । गतः सूक्तविनियोगः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

In the fourth Anuvāka there are six Sūktas. Of them the first Sūkta, यो रयिव इति (*yo rayiva iti*), has twenty-four Ṛks; its seer is Śaṃyu, the son of Bṛhaspati, and its deity is Indra. From the beginning the first six Ṛks are Anuṣṭubh; the seventh, eighth and ninth are Virāj, or the eighth alone is Virāj; the rest are Triṣṭubh. So the Anukramaṇī says: यो रयिवश्चतुर्विंशतिः शंयुर्बार्हस्पत्यो ह्यादौ षळनुष्टुभस्तिस्रश्च विराजो मध्यमैव वासामिति । (*yo rayivaścaturviṃśatiḥ śaṃyurbārhaspatyo hyādau ṣaḷanuṣṭubhastisraśca virājo madhyamaiva vāsāmiti |*). The use of the Sūkta has been stated already.

Anuvāda: In the fourth Anuvāka of the sixth Maṇḍala there are six Sūktas. Of them this Sūkta beginning यो रयिवः (*yo rayivaḥ*) is the first. It has twenty-four Ṛks. The seer of this Sūkta is Śaṃyu, the son of Bṛhaspati; the deity is Indra. In this Sūkta the first six Ṛks are in the Anuṣṭubh metre; the seventh, eighth and ninth Ṛks are in the Virāj metre; the eighth Ṛk is in Virāj or Triṣṭubh; all the remaining Ṛks are in the Triṣṭubh metre. In the Anukramaṇī it is said: यो रयिवश्चतुर्विंशतिः शंयुर्बार्हस्पत्यो ह्यादौ षळनुष्टुभस्तिप्रश्च विराजो मध्यमैव वासां (*yo rayivaścaturviṃśatiḥ śaṃyurbārhaspatyo hyādau ṣaḷanuṣṭubhastipraśca virājo madhyamaiva vāsāṃ*). The use of the Sūkta has been stated before.

Sūkta—44
Maṇḍala—6. Anuvāka—4. Sūkta—44.
Aṣṭaka—4. Adhyāya—7. Varga—16, 17, 18, 19, 20.
Number of Ṛks in the Sūkta—24.
Ṛṣi—शंयुर्बार्हस्पत्यः (*śaṃyurbārhaspatyaḥ*).
Devatā—इंद्रः (*iṃdraḥ*).
Chandas—Ṛks 1-6 Ānuṣṭubha; 7, 9 Virāj; 8 Virāj or Triṣṭubh; Ṛks **NOTE** Triṣṭubh.

### Printed p. 103

#### Ṛk 1

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> यो र॑यिवो र॒यिंत॑मो॒ यो द्यु॒म्नैर्द्यु॒म्न व॑त्तमः ।  
> सोमः॑ सु॒तः स इं॑द्र॒ तेऽस्ति॑ स्वधापते॒ मदः॑ ॥ १ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> यः । रयिऽवः । रयिंतमः । यः । द्युम्नैः । द्युम्नवत् ऽतमः ।  
> सोमः । सुतः । सः । इंद्र । ते । अस्ति । स्वधापते । मदः ॥ १ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे रयिवो धनवन्निंद्र यः सोमो रयिंतमोऽतिशयेन रयिमान्
> धनवान् । यश्च द्युम्नैर्द्योतमानैर्यशोभिर्द्युम्नवत्तमोऽतिशयेन यशस्वी
> हे स्वधापते स्वधाया अन्नस्य सोमलक्षणस्य पालकेंद्र स सोमोऽभिषुतः सन् ते
> तव मदो मदकरोऽस्ति । भवति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O wealthy Indra, the Soma that is exceedingly rich, and that with its shining glories is exceedingly glorious: O lord of Svadhā, protector of the Soma-food, that Soma, being pressed, is exhilarating for you.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- रयिवः (*rayivaḥ*)—O wealthy one
- स्वधापते (*svadhāpate*)—O protector of the Soma in the form of Svadhā
- इंद्र (*iṃdra*)—O Indra
- यः (*yaḥ*)—which Soma
- रयिंतमः (*rayiṃtamaḥ*)—is exceedingly wealthy
- यः (*yaḥ*)—which Soma
- द्युम्नैः (*dyumnaiḥ*)—by its splendours
- द्युम्नवत्तम (*dyumnavattama*)—is exceedingly radiant and glorious
- सः सोमः (*saḥ somaḥ*)—that same Soma
- सुतः (*sutaḥ*)—pressed
- ते (*te*)—for you
- मदः (*madaḥ*)—a cause of joy
- अस्ति (*asti*)—is

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O wealthy Indra, protector of the Soma in the form of Svadhā, the Soma that is exceedingly rich, and that by its splendours is exceedingly radiant and glorious, that same Soma, being pressed, is a cause of joy to you.

##### English translation
Opulent Indra, the Soma that abounds with riches, and is most
resplendent with glories, is poured out: is is your exhilaration, Indra,
lord of the offering.

### Printed p. 104

#### Ṛk 2

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> यः श॒ग्मस्तु॑विशग्म ते रा॒यो दा॒मा म॑ती॒नां ।  
> सोमः॑ सु॒तः स इं॑द्र॒ तेऽस्ति॑ स्वधापते॒ मदः॑ ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> यः । शग्मः । तुविशग्म । ते । रायः । दामा । मतीनां ।  
> सोमः । सुतः । सः । इंद्र । ते । अस्ति । स्वधापते । मदः ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे तुविशग्म बहुसुखेंद्र यः शग्मः सुखकरस्ते त्वदीयो यः सोमो
> मतीनां स्तोतृणां रायो धनस्य दामा दाता भवति हे स्वधापत इंद्र स
> सोमोऽभिषुतस्तव मदकरो भवति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Indra of much happiness, that Soma of yours which is the maker of happiness, and which is the giver of wealth to the praisers, to the singers of hymns, that Soma, O lord of Svadhā, being pressed, is exhilarating for you.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- तुविशग्म (*tuviśagma*)—O you who have happiness of many kinds
- स्वधापते (*svadhāpate*)—O protector of the Soma in the form of Svadhā
- इंद्र (*iṃdra*)—O Indra
- यः (*yaḥ*)—which Soma
- शग्मः (*śagmaḥ*)—is productive of happiness
- ते (*te*)—your
- मतीनां (*matīnāṃ*)—to the praisers
- रायः (*rāyaḥ*)—wealth
- दामा (*dāmā*)—and is the giver
- सः सोमः (*saḥ somaḥ*)—that same Soma
- सुतः (*sutaḥ*)—pressed
- ते (*te*)—for you
- मदः (*madaḥ*)—a cause of joy
- अस्ति (*asti*)—is

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, who have happiness of many kinds and are the protector of the Soma in the form of Svadhā, the Soma that is productive of happiness and gives wealth to your praisers, that same Soma, being pressed, is a cause of joy to you.

##### English translation
The Soma, possessor of felicity, which gives you delight, and which
is the bestower of riches on your votaries, is poured out : it is your
exhilaration, Indra, lord of the offering.
