#### Ṛk 3

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> येन॑ वृ॒द्दो न शव॑सा तु॒रो न स्वाभि॑रू॒तिभिः॑ ।  
> सोमः॑ सु॒तः स इं॑द्र॒ तेऽस्ति॑ स्वधापते॒ मदः॑ ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> येन । वृद्धः । न । शवसा । तुरः । न । स्वाभिः । ऊतिभिः ।  
> सोमः । सुतः । सः । इंद्र । ते । अस्ति । स्वधापते । मदः ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> इमौ नशब्दौ समुच्चयार्थौ । येन पीतेन सोमेन वृद्धो न प्रवृद्धश्च
> सन् स्वाभिरूतिभिरात्मीयैर्मरुद्भिः सार्धं शवसा बलेन तुरः शत्रूणां हिंसकश्च
> भवसि स सोम इत्यादि पूर्ववत् ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The two words न (*na*) have the sense of conjunction. By drinking which Soma you become both grown and strong, and together with your own helpers, your own Maruts, with strength, you become the destroyer of enemies. That Soma, and so on, as before.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- स्वधापते (*svadhāpate*)—O protector of the Soma in the form of Svadhā
- इंद्र (*iṃdra*)—O Indra
- येन (*yena*)—by drinking which Soma juice
- वृद्यो न (*vṛdyo na*)—you grow to full strength
- स्वाभिः (*svābhiḥ*)—your companions
- वातिभिः (*vātibhiḥ*)—with the Maruts
- शवसा (*śavasā*)—and with strength, joined
- तुरः (*turaḥ*)—you become the destroyer of enemies
- सः सोमः (*saḥ somaḥ*)—that same Soma
- सुतः (*sutaḥ*)—pressed
- ते (*te*)—for you
- मदः (*madaḥ*)—a cause of joy
- अस्ति (*asti*)—is

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, protector of the Soma in the form of Svadhā, by drinking which Soma juice you grow to full strength and, joined with your companions the Maruts and with strength, become the destroyer of enemies, that same Soma, being pressed, is a cause of joy to you.

##### English translation
The Soma, whereby you are augmented in strength, and together with your defenders (the Maruts), are victorious (over your foes), is poured out: it is your exhilaration, Indra, lord of the offering.

### Printed p. 106

#### Ṛk 4

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> त्यमु॑ वो॒ अप्र॑हणं गृणी॒षे शव॑स॒स्पतिं॑ ।  
> इंद्रं॑ विश्वा॒ साहं॒ नरं॒ मंहि॑ष्ठं वि॒श्वच॑र्षणिं ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> त्यं । ऊं इति । वः । अप्रहनं । गृणीषे । शवसः । पतिं ।  
> इंद्रं । विश्वसहं । नरं । मंहिष्ठं । विश्वचर्षणिं ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> पृष्ठ षडहस्य चतुर्थेऽहनि प्रउगशस्त्रे त्यमु व इत्यैंद्रस्तृचः । सूत्रितं च ।
> त्यमु वो अप्रहणमप त्यं वृजिनं रिपुं । आ. ७-११ । इति ॥
> हे ऋत्विग्यजमाना वो युष्मदर्थं त्यमु तमेवेंद्रं गृणीषे । स्तौमि ।
> यद्वा । वो यूयं गृणीषे। गृणीत। स्तुत॥ वचनव्यत्ययः। कीदृशमिंद्रं। अप्रहणमप्रहंतारं भक्तानामनुग्राहकं शवसो बलस्य पतिं पालकं विश्वासाहं विश्वस्य
> शत्रोरभिभवितारं नरं नेतारं मंहिष्ठं दातृतमं विश्वचर्षणिं सर्वस्य द्रष्टारं ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

On the fourth day of the Pṛṣṭha-ṣaḍaha, in the Praüga-śastra, the tṛca त्यमु व (*tyamu va*) is addressed to Indra. It is laid down in the sūtra: त्यमु वो अप्रहणमप त्यं वृजिनं रिपुं (*tyamu vo aprahaṇamapa tyaṃ vṛjinaṃ ripuṃ*) (Ā. 7-11). O Ṛtvijs and sacrificers, for you I praise that very Indra. Or else: you praise him. What kind of Indra? अप्रहणं (*aprahaṇaṃ*), one who does not strike, the favourer of his devotees; the lord पतिं (*patiṃ*) and protector of strength; विश्वासाहं (*viśvāsāhaṃ*), the conqueror of every enemy; नरं (*naraṃ*), the leader; मंहिष्ठं (*maṃhiṣṭhaṃ*), the most generous giver; विश्वचर्षणिं (*viśvacarṣaṇiṃ*), the beholder of everything.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- (O Ṛtvijs and sacrificers) अप्रहणं (*aprahaṇaṃ*)—who favours his devotees without harming them
- शवसः (*śavasaḥ*)—of strength
- पतिं (*patiṃ*)—the protector
- विश्वासाहं (*viśvāsāhaṃ*)—who strikes all enemies
- नरं (*naraṃ*)—the leader
- मंहिष्ठं (*maṃhiṣṭhaṃ*)—the generous giver
- विश्वचर्षणिं (*viśvacarṣaṇiṃ*)—who has the power to see everything
- त्यमु इंद्रं (*tyamu iṃdraṃ*)—that same Indra
- वः (*vaḥ*)—for your welfare
- गृणीषे (*gṛṇīṣe*)—I praise

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Ṛtvijs and sacrificers, for your welfare I praise that same Indra, who favours his devotees without harming them, who is the leader and the generous giver, and who has the power to see everything.

##### English translation
(Worshippers), for you I glorify that Indra who disappoints not
(his adorers); the lord of strength, the all-subduing, the leader (of rites),
the most beautiful, the beholder of the universe.

### Printed p. 107

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

On the fourth day of the sacrifice called Pṛṣṭha-ṣaḍaha, when the Praüga-śastra mantras are recited, the three Ṛks beginning त्र्यमुवः (*tryamuvaḥ*) are to be recited as the tṛca addressed to the deity Indra; this is explained by the sūtra of the Āśvalāyana Śrauta Sūtra, त्यमु वो अप्रहणमप त्यं वृजिनं रिपुं (*tyamu vo aprahaṇamapa tyaṃ vṛjinaṃ ripuṃ*) (Ā. 7-11).

#### Ṛk 5

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> यं व॒र्धयं॒तीद्गिरः॒ पतिं॑ तु॒रस्य॒ राध॑सः ।  
> तमिन्न॑स्य॒ रोद॑सी दे॒वी शुष्मं॑ सपर्यतः ॥ ५ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> यं । वर्धयंति । इत् । गिरः । पतिं । तुरस्य । राधसः ।  
> तं । इत् । नु । अस्य । रोदसी इति । देवी इति । शुष्कं । सपर्यतः ॥ ५ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> गिरः स्तुतय इंद्रसंबंधिनं यमित् यमेव शुष्मं बलं वर्धयंति ।
> कीदृशं । पतिमीश्वरं । कस्य । तुरस्य हिंसकस्य शत्रो राधसो धनस्य । शत्रुधनानामपहर्तारमित्यर्थः । अस्येंद्रस्य तमित्तमेव शुष्मं शोषकं बलं देवी देवनशीले
> रोदसी द्यावापृथिव्यौ नु क्षिप्रं सपर्यतः । परिचरतः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The hymns of praise increase that very strength शुष्मं (*śuṣmaṃ*), the strength that belongs to Indra. Of what kind is he? He is the lord, the master, of whom? Of the harming enemy and of his wealth राधसः (*rādhasaḥ*); that is, he is the taker away of the wealth of enemies. That very drying, consuming strength of this Indra the two divine, shining ones, heaven and earth, quickly attend upon, that is, serve.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- तुरस्य (*turasya*)—of the harming enemy
- राधसः (*rādhasaḥ*)—of the wealth
- पतिं (*patiṃ*)—the lord
- यं इत् (*yaṃ it*)—whose very strength
- गिरः (*giraḥ*)—the hymns of praise
- वर्धयंति (*vardhayaṃti*)—cause to grow
- अस्य तं इत् तुष्मं (*asya taṃ it tuṣmaṃ*)—of that Indra, that same strength
- देवी (*devī*)—the divine ones
- रोदसी (*rodasī*)—heaven and earth
- नु (*nu*)—quickly
- सपर्यतः (*saparyataḥ*)—worship

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

The strength of that Indra, the lord of the wealth of the harming enemy, which the hymns of praise cause to grow, that same strength of that Indra the divine heaven and earth quickly worship.

### Printed p. 108

##### English translation
The divine heaven and earth adore that vigour of his, which our
hymns augment, the appropriator of the riches of the foe.

#### Ṛk 6

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> तद्व॑ उ॒क्तस्य॑ ब॒र्हणेंद्रा॑योपस्तृणी॒षणि॑ ।  
> विपो॒ न यस्को॒तयो॒ वि यद्रोहं॑ति स॒क्षितः॑ ॥ ६ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> तत् । वः । उक्तस्य । बर्हणा । इंद्राय । उपस्तृणीषणि ।  
> विपः । न । यस्य । ऊतयः । वि । यत् । रोहंति । सक्षितः ॥ ६ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे स्तोतारो वो युष्मदीयस्योक्तस्य स्तोत्रस्य तद्बर्हणा बहुत्वं
> माहात्म्यमिंद्रायेंद्रार्थमुपस्तृणीषणि । उपस्तरणीयं । उपेत्य विस्तरणीयं।
> इंद्रो यथा स्तोत्रस्य माहात्म्यं जानीयात् तथा विस्तार्यत इत्यर्थः ।
> यस्येंद्रस्योतयो रक्षा विपो न मेधाविन इव भवंति । सर्वकार्यकुशला
> इत्यर्थः । यद्वा नेति पूरकः । विपो विप्रस्य मेधाविनो यस्येंद्रस्यॆति योज्यं ।
> यद्यस्मिंश्चेंद्रे सक्षितः समाननिवासाः संहता ऊतयो वि रोहंति प्रादुर्भवंति
> तस्मा इंद्रायेत्यर्थः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O praisers, the greatness बर्हणा (*barhaṇā*), that is, the abundance and majesty, of your uttered praise is to be spread out for Indra, to be extended for him, so that Indra may know the majesty of the praise. His protections ऊतयः (*ūtayaḥ*) are like the wise ones विपः (*vipaḥ*), that is, skilled in every work. Or else: the word न (*na*) is a mere filler, and विपः (*vipaḥ*) is to be connected with the wise Indra, of whom the protections are meant. In whom the united, gathered protections arise and appear, for that Indra the praise is to be spread.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- (O praisers) यस्य (*yasya*)—of which Indra
- ऊतयः (*ūtayaḥ*)—the protections
- विपो न (*vipo na*)—are, like the wise, competent for all works
- यत् (*yat*)—in which Indra
- सक्षितः (*sakṣitaḥ*)—the gathered protections
- वि रोहंति (*vi rohaṃti*)—arise; for such an
- इंद्राय (*iṃdrāya*)—for Indra
- वः (*vaḥ*)—your
- उक्तस्य (*uktasya*)—of the praise
- तत् बर्हणा (*tat barhaṇā*)—that same greatness
- उपस्तृणीषणि (*upastṛṇīṣaṇi*)—may be extended and made to grow

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O praisers, for such an Indra, whose protections, like the wise, are competent for all works, and in whom the protections contained in him arise, may that same greatness of your praise be extended and made to grow.

### Printed p. 109

##### English translation
(Worshippers), the efficacy of your eulogy is to be manifested to that
Indra, whose protections. iike (those) of a sensible man. are displayed as
abiding along with him.

#### Ṛk 7

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अवि॑द॒द्दक्षं॑ मि॒त्रो नवी॑यान्पपा॒नो दे॒वेभ्यो॒ वस्को॑  
> अच्चैत् ।  
> स॒स॒वान्सौ॒ लाभि॑र्धौ॒तरी॑भिरुरु॒ष्या पा॒युर॑भव॒त्सखि॑भ्यः ॥ ७ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अविदत् । दक्षं । मित्रः । नवीयान् । पपानः । देवेभ्यः । वस्यः । अचैत् ।  
> सस्यवान् । स्तौलाभिः । धौतरीभिः । उरुष्या । पायुः । अभवत् । सखिभ्यः ॥ ७ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> स इंद्रो दक्षं कर्मसु समर्थं प्रवृद्धं वा यष्टारमविदत् । जानाति ।
> कीदृशः । मित्रो मित्रभूतो नवीयान्नवतरः कल्याणतरः स्तुत्यतरो वा । स च
> पपानः सोमं पिबन् देवेभ्यः स्तोतृभ्यो वस्यो वसीयः श्रेष्ठं धनमचैत् ।
> जिनोति । उपचितं करोति । ददातीति यावत् । अपि च ससवान् । ससमित्यन्ननाम ।
> हविर्लक्षणान्नोपेतः स इंद्रः स्तौलाभिः स्फूलाभिः प्रवृद्धाभिर्धौतरीभिः कंपनकारिणीभिर्वडवाभिरीदृशैर्मरुद्भिर्वा युक्तः सन् सखिभ्यः स्तोतृभ्य उरुष्या रक्षणेच्छयागत्य पायुरभवत् । रक्षको भवति । यद्वा । ससवानिति सनतेः क्वसौ रूपं ।
> स्तौलाभिर्धौतरीभिः ससवान् संभजमान इति संबंधः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

That Indra knows the दक्षं (*dakṣaṃ*), the sacrificer who is competent in rites or who is grown great. Of what kind is Indra? A friend, नवीयान् (*navīyān*), newer, that is, more auspicious or more praiseworthy. And he, drinking the Soma, accumulates, that is, gives, for the gods, the praisers, the best wealth वस्यः (*vasyaḥ*). Moreover he is ससवान् (*sasavān*): सस (*sasa*) is a name for food, so he is provided with the food in the form of the oblation. That Indra, joined with the stout, grown mares that cause shaking, or with Maruts of such a kind, comes with desire to protect the praisers, his friends, and becomes their protector. Or else: ससवान् (*sasavān*) means partaking; it is connected with the stout and shaking ones.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- (Indra) दक्षं (*dakṣaṃ*)—the sacrificer who is competent in rites
- अविदत् (*avidat*)—knows
- मित्रः (*mitraḥ*)—friend to all
- नवीयान् (*navīyān*)—and worshipful Indra
- पपानः (*papānaḥ*)—drinking Soma
- देवेभ्यः (*devebhyaḥ*)—to the praisers
- वस्यः (*vasyaḥ*)—the best wealth
- अचैत् (*acait*)—gives
- ससवान् (*sasavān*)—Indra, provided with food
- स्तौलाभिः (*staulābhiḥ*)—with growing

### Printed p. 110

- धौतरीभिः (*dhautarībhiḥ*)—horses, joined with them**NOTE**
- सखिभ्यः (*sakhibhyaḥ*)—to the praisers
- उरुष्या (*uruṣyā*)—coming with the desire to help
- पायुः (*pāyuḥ*)—as their protector
- अभवत् (*abhavat*)—becomes

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

Indra knows who the sacrificer is that is competent in rites. Indra, who is a friend to all and worshipful, gives, drinking Soma, the best wealth to the praisers. Indra, provided with food, joined with growing horses, comes with the desire to help the praisers and becomes their protector.

##### English translation
Indra appreciates him who is skilled (in holy rites): a recent friend,
quaffing (the libation), he heaps excellent (wealth) upon the devout :
partaking of (the sacrificial) food, (and brought) by his robust agitators
(of the earth, his steeds), he, through his benevolence, is a protector to
his friends.

#### Ṛk 8

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> ऋ॒तस्य॑ प॒थि वे॒धा अ॑पायि श्रि॒ये मनां॑सि दे॒वासो॑ अक्रन् ।  
> दधा॑नो॒ नाम॑ म॒हो वचो॑भि॒र्वपु॑र्द्वृ॒शये॑ वे॒न्यो व्या॑वः ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> ऋतस्य । पथि । वेधाः । आसायि । श्रिये । मनांसि । देवासः । अक्रन् ।  
> दधानः । नाम । महः । वचभिः । वपुः । दृशये । वेन्यः । वि । आवरित्यावः ॥ ८ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> ऋतस्य यज्ञस्य पथि मार्गे वेधा विधाता सर्वस्य द्रष्टा सोमोऽपायि ।
> इंद्रेण पीतोऽभूत् । यद्वा । वेधा विधाते०द्रोऽपायि । सोमं पिबतु ॥
> व्यत्ययेन कर्तरि चिन् ॥ तस्मिन् सोम इंद्रस्य मनांसि श्रिये श्रयितुं देवाः
> स्तोतार ऋत्विजोऽक्रन् । कर्माणि कुर्वंति । स चेंद्रो नाम शत्रूणां नामकं

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

On the path of the sacrifice the Soma, the वेधा (*vedhā*), the arranger and beholder of all, has been drunk by Indra. Or else: Indra, the arranger, has been made to drink, that is, let him drink the Soma. The sentence is explained by a change of form. In that Soma, to resort to it, the gods, that is, the praisers and priests, have made Indra's minds, that is, they perform the rites. And that Indra, so named because he humbles enemies,

### Printed p. 111

> महो महदात्मीयं वपुः शरीरं दधानो धारयन् वचोऽभिः स्तुतिभिर्वेन्यू
> वननीयः संभजनीयश्च सन् दृशये दर्शनार्थं व्यावः । विवृणोतु । प्रकाशयतु ।
> अनुकूलं तेज इति शेषः । यद्वा । वेन्य इति वेनतेः कांतिकर्मणो रूपं वपुरिति
> च रूपनाम । वेन्यः कमनीयो वपुर्भास्वरं रूपं दृशये दर्शनार्थं व्यावः
> विवृणोतु वचोऽभिः स्तूयमानः सन् ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

bearing his own great body वपुः (*vapuḥ*), being adorable and to be resorted to by praises, may he uncover, that is, make manifest his favourable splendour, for the sake of being seen; the word "splendour" is to be supplied. Or else: वेन्यः (*venyaḥ*) is a form of the root वेनति (*venati*), which means to shine and to desire, and वपुः (*vapuḥ*) is a name for form: Indra, the desirable, being praised with words, may uncover his radiant form, for the sake of being seen.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- ऋतस्य (*ṛtasya*)—of the sacrifice
- पथि (*pathi*)—on the path
- वेधाः (*vedhāḥ*)—Indra, the all-seeing (the creator)
- अपायि (*apāyi*)—may he drink the Soma
- मनांसि (*manāṃsi*)—the mind of Indra
- श्रिये (*śriye*)—to gladden
- देवासः (*devāsaḥ*)—the praisers
- अक्रन् (*akran*)—perform the rite
- (Indra) नाम (*nāma*)—who brings enemies down
- महः (*mahaḥ*)—and is great
- वपुः (*vapuḥ*)—body
- दधानः (*dadhānaḥ*)—possessing
- वचोभिः (*vacobhiḥ*)—by praises
- वेन्यः (*venyaḥ*)—worshipped
- दृशये (*dṛśaye*)—to be visible to all
- व्यावः (*vyāvaḥ*)—may he shine forth

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

May Indra, the all-seeing**NOTE**, drink the Soma on the path of the sacrifice. The praisers perform the rite to gladden the mind of Indra. Possessing a body that brings enemies down and is great, and worshipped by praises, may Indra shine forth and appear, so as to be visible to all.

##### English translation
The Soma, creator (of all), on the path of sacrifice, has been drunk:
the worshippers have presented it to gratify the mind (of Indra): may he,
the humilator (of his foes), possessing a vast body, propitiated by our
praises, become manifest to our view.

#### Ṛk 9

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> द्यु॒मत्त॑मं॒ दक्षं॑ धेह्य॒स्मे सेधा॒ जना॑नां पू॒र्वीररा॑तीः ।  
> वर्षी॑यो॒ वयः॑ कृणुहि॒ शची॑भि॒र्धन॑स्य सा॒ताव॒स्मा अ॑विद्धि ॥९॥

### Printed p. 112

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> द्युमत्कतमं । दक्षं । धेहि । अस्मे इति । सेध । जनानां । पूर्वीः । अरातीः ।  
> वर्षीयः । वयः । कृणुहि । शचीभिः । धनस्य । सात् । अस्मान् । अविद्धि ॥ ९ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे इंद्र द्युमत्तमं दीप्तिमत्तमं दक्षं बलमस्मे अस्मासु धेहि । धारय ।
> जनानां स्तोत्कणामस्माकं पूर्वीर्बह्वररातीः शत्रुसेनाः सेध । निषेध ।
> निवारय । अपि चास्माकं वर्षीयो वृद्धतरु वयोऽन्नं शचीभिरात्मीयाभिः प्रज्ञाभिः
> कृणिहि । कुरु । तथा धनस्य सातौ संभजनेऽस्मानविड्डि । गमय । यद्वा । धनस्य
> संभजनार्थमस्मान्रक्ष ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Indra, place in us the most brilliant strength दक्षं (*dakṣaṃ*). Keep off, hold back, the many hostile armies of us the praisers. Moreover make our food वयः (*vayaḥ*) more abundant by your own wisdom शचीभिः (*śacībhiḥ*). And in the winning of wealth, lead us, that is, make us go. Or else: protect us for the sharing in wealth.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- (O Indra) द्युमत्तमं (*dyumattamaṃ*)—most brilliant
- दक्षं (*dakṣaṃ*)—strength
- अस्मे (*asme*)—to us
- धेहि (*dhehi*)—give
- जनानां (*janānāṃ*)—for us the praisers
- पूर्वीः (*pūrvīḥ*)—many
- अरातीः (*arātīḥ*)—hostile armies
- सेध (*sedha*)—destroy
- शचीभिः (*śacībhiḥ*)—to us who are engaged in rites**NOTE**
- वर्षीयः (*varṣīyaḥ*)—best
- वयः (*vayaḥ*)—food
- कृणुहि (*kṛṇuhi*)—make
- धनस्य (*dhanasya*)—of wealth
- सातौ (*sātau*)—for the gain
- अस्मान् (*asmān*)—us
- अविड्डि (*aviḍḍi*)—protect

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, give us the most brilliant strength. Destroy the many hostile armies for us the praisers. Make the best food for us who are engaged in rites. Protect us so that we obtain wealth.

##### English translation
Bestow upon us most brilliant vigour : oppose the numerous enemies
of your worshippers: grant us, for our pious acts, abundant food : secure
us in the enjoyment of wealth.

#### Ṛk 10

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> इंद्र॒ तुभ्य॒मिन्म॑घवन्नभूम व॒यं दा॒त्रे ह॑रिवो॒ मा वि  ॑
> वेनः ।  
> नकि॑रा॒पिर्॑दृशे मर्त्य॒त्रा किमं॒ग र॑ध्र॒चोद॑नं त्वाहुः॥१०॥

### Printed p. 114

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> इंद्र । तुभ्यं । इत् । मघवन् । अभूम । वयं । दात्रे । हरिवः । मा । वि । वेनः ।  
> नकिः । आपिः । ददृशे । मर्त्यत्रा । किं । अंग । रध्रचोदनं । त्वा । आहुः ॥ १० ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे मघवन् धनवन्निंद्र दात्रे कामानां दात्रे तुभ्यमित् तुभ्यमेव वयं
> शेषभूता अभूम । यद्वा । त्वदर्थमेव दात्रे हविषां दाने वर्तमाना अभूम ।
> हे हरिवो हरिवन्निंद्र अतः कारणान्मा वि वेनः । मा विगतकामो भूः । आस्मान्
> सर्वदा कामयस्व ॥ वेनतिः कांतिकर्मा ॥ मर्त्यत्रा मर्त्येषु मनुष्येषु मध्य
> आपिर्बंधुस्त्वत्तोऽन्यः कश्चिदपि नकिर्ददृशे । नैव दृश्यते । किमनेन विशेषकथनेन ।
> अंग हे इंद्र त्वा त्वां रध्रचोदनं रध्रस्य राधकस्य समृद्धस्य धनस्य चोदनं
> चोदयितारमाहुः कथयंति पुराजाः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Maghavan, wealthy Indra, to you the giver of desires, to you alone we have become subordinate. Or else: for you alone we have become engaged in giving oblations. O lord of bay horses, for this reason do not be without desire; desire us always; the root वेनति (*venati*) has the sense of shining and desiring. Among mortals, among men, no other kinsman than you is seen at all. What use is this particular statement? O dear Indra, the ancients call you the impeller रध्रचोदनं (*radhracodanaṃ*) of prosperous, abundant wealth.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- मघवन् (*maghavan*)—O wealthy
- इंद्र (*iṃdra*)—Indra
- दात्रे (*dātre*)—giver of desires
- तुभ्यमित् (*tubhyamit*)—for your service alone
- वयं (*vayaṃ*)—we
- अभूम (*abhūma*)—are ready
- हरिवः (*harivaḥ*)—O Indra, possessor of horses
- मा वि वेनः (*mā vi venaḥ*)—do not forget us
- मर्त्यत्रा (*martyatrā*)—among men
- आपिः (*āpiḥ*)—a kinsman (other than you)
- नकिः ददृशे (*nakiḥ dadṛśe*)—no one has been seen
- अंग (*aṃga*)—O Indra
- त्वा (*tvā*)—you
- रथ्रचोदनं (*rathracodanaṃ*)—as the impeller of wealth
- अहुः (*ahuḥ*)—they have praised

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O wealthy Indra, we are ready for your service alone, you who grant desires. O Indra, possessor of horses, do not forget us. Other than you, we have seen no kinsman among men. O Indra, everyone has praised you alone as the impeller of wealth and the giver of wealth.

##### English translation
Indra, possessor of affluence, we have recourse to you, the bountiful lord of steeds, be not unfavourable to us: no other kinsman is beheld (by us) amongst men: why else have they called you the bestower of wealth?

#### Ṛk 11
**NOTE**

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> मा जस्व॑ने वृषभ नो ररीथा॒ मा ते॑ रे॒वतः॑ स॒ख्ये रि॑षाम ।  
> पू॒र्वीष्ट॑ इंद्र नि॒ष्पिधो॒ जने॑षु ज॒ह्यसु॑स्वी॒न्र वृ॒हापृ॑णतः ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> मा । जस्वने । वृषभ । नः । ररीथाः । मा । ते । रेवतः । सख्ये । रिषाम ॥  
> पूर्विः । ते । इंद्र । निःसिधः । जनेषु । जहि । असुस्वीन् । प्र । वृह । अपृणतः ॥१॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे वृषभ कामानां वर्षितरिंद्र जस्वने ॥ जसिरुपक्षयकर्मा ॥ उपक्षपयित्रे
> राक्षसादये नोऽस्मान् मा ररीथाः । मा दाः । रेवतो धनवतस्ते तव सख्ये
> सखित्वे वर्तमाना वयं मा रिषाम । हिंसिता मा भूम । हे इंद्र ते त्वदीयाः
> पूर्वीर्बह्निर्निष्पिधो निषेधा निवारणानि जनेषु शत्रुषु निहिताः संति । यद्वा ।
> निष्पिधो निषेधार्था रज्जवः । ताः शत्रुजनेषु तव बह्वो विद्यंते । आतः कारणादसुष्टीननभिषोत्थनयजमानान् जहि । मारय । अपृणतो हवींष्यप्रयच्छतश्च
> प्र वृह । उन्मूलय ॥ पृण दान इति धातुः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Vṛṣabha, showerer of desires, Indra, do not give us up to the destroyer जस्वने (*jasvane*); the root जसि (*jasi*) has the sense of destroying; that is, to a destroyer such as a Rākṣasa. Being in the friendship of you, the wealthy, may we not be harmed. O Indra, your many hindrances, that is, restraints, are set among the enemy people. Or else: the hindrances are ropes for restraining, and many of them are found among your enemy people. For this reason slay those who do not press the Soma and uproot those who do not give oblations.**NOTE**

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- वृषभ (*vṛṣabha*)—O Indra, showerer of desires
- जस्वने (*jasvane*)—to the harming enemy
- नः (*naḥ*)—us
- मा ररीथाः (*mā rarīthāḥ*)—do not give up
- रेवतः (*revataḥ*)—of the wealthy
- ते (*te*)—your
- सख्ये (*sakhye*)—in friendship, we who are
- मा रिषाम (*mā riṣāma*)—do not let us be harmed
- इंद्र (*iṃdra*)—O Indra
- ते (*te*)—your
- पूर्वीः (*pūrvīḥ*)—many
- निष्पिधः (*niṣpidhaḥ*)—hindrances
- जनेषु (*janeṣu*)—are among the enemy people
- असुष्टीन् (*asuṣṭīn*)—those who do not press the Soma
- जहि (*jahi*)—destroy
- अपृणितः (*apṛṇitaḥ*)—those who do not offer oblations
- प्र वृह (*pra vṛha*)—destroy

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, showerer of desires, do not give us up to the harming enemy. Do not let us, who are in the friendship of you, the wealthy, be harmed. O Indra, many hindrances of yours are among the enemy people. Destroy those who do not press the Soma and those who do not offer oblations.

### Printed p. 115

##### English translation
Give us not up, showerer (of benefits), to the obstructor (of our rites) : relying upon the friendship of you, lord of riches, may we be unharmed : many are the hindrances (opposed) to you amongst men: slay those who make no libations, root out those who present no offering.

#### Ṛk 12 **NOTE**

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> उद॒भ्राणी॑व स्त॒नय॑न्निय॒र्तिंद्रो॒ राधां॒स्यश्वा॑नि॒ गव्या॑ ।  
> त्वम॑सि प्र॒दिवः॑ का॒रुधा॑या॒ मा त्वा॑दा॒मान॒ आ द॑भन्म॒घोनः॑ ॥  
> ॥ चरित्रॆ ॥  
> उत् । अभ्राणिइव । स्तनयन् । इयर्ति । इंद्रः । राधांसि । अश्वानि । गव्या ॥  
> त्वं । अ॑सि । प्र॒दिवः॑ । का॒रुधा॑याः॒ । मा । त्वा॑ । आदा॒मानः॒ । आ । द॑भन् । म॒घोनः॑ ।

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> अभ्राणीव स्तनयन् गर्जयन् पर्जन्यो यथा मेघानुद्गमयत्येवमिंद्रोऽश्वान्यश्वसंबंधीनि गव्या गव्यानि गोसंबंधीन्वेतत्समूहद्वयरूपाणि
> राधांसि धनान्युदियर्ति । उदीरयति । उद्गमयति स्तोतृभ्यो दातुं । उत्तरार्धः
> प्रत्यक्षकृतः । हे इंद्र प्रदिवः पुराणस्त्वं कारुधायाः कारूणां स्तोतृणां
> धारयितासि । भवसि । तादृशं त्वा त्वां मघोनो धनवंतः कृपणा अदामानो
> हविषामदाकारो मा दभन् । मा हिंसिषुः । यद्वा वचनव्यत्ययः । मघवानं
> धनवंतं त्वामिति संबंधः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Like thundering Parjanya who raises up the clouds, Indra raises up and sends forth riches, both of horses and of cattle, in order to give them to the worshippers. The second half of the verse is addressed directly: O Indra, ancient one, you are the upholder of the कारूणां (*kārūṇāṃ*), the praisers. Such as you are, let the wealthy, the niggardly ones who offer no oblations, not harm you. Or else there is a change of number, and the construction is: let them not harm you, the wealthy one.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- स्तनयन् (*stanayan*)—the thundering Parjanya
- अभ्राणीव (*abhrāṇīva*)—as he scatters the clouds in masses upwards (disperses them)
- इंद्रः (*iṃdraḥ*)—Indra
- अश्वानि (*aśvāni*)—those connected with horses
- गव्या (*gavyā*)—and those full of cattle
- राधांसि (*rādhāṃsi*)—riches
- उदियर्ति (*udiyarti*)—he spreads out abundantly (O Indra)
- प्रदिवः (*pradivaḥ*)—ancient one
- त्वं (*tvaṃ*)—you yourself
- कारुधायाः (*kārudhāyāḥ*)—as the upholder of the praisers
- आसि (*āsi*)—you are (such)
- त्वा (*tvā*)—you
- मघोनः (*maghonaḥ*)—the wealthy, niggardly ones
- अदामानः (*adāmānaḥ*)—not offering oblations
- मा आ धभन् (*mā ā dhabhan*)—let them not harm

### Printed p. 116

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

As the thundering Parjanya scatters the clouds upwards in masses (disperses them), Indra spreads out abundantly riches full of horses and full of cattle. O Indra, you yourself, the ancient one, are the upholder of the praisers. Let the niggardly ones, who though wealthy offer no oblations, not harm such a one as you.

##### English translation
As Indra, when thundering, raises the clouds, so he (heaps upon his worshippers) riches of horses and cattle: you, Indra, are the ancient upholder of the sacrificer : let not the opulent wrong you, not presenting (oblations).

#### Ṛk 13

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अध्व॑यो वीर॒ प्रम॒हे सु॒ताना॒मिंद्रा॑य भर॒ सह्य॑स्य॒ राजा॑।  
> यः पू॒र्व्याभि॑रु॒त नूत॑नाभिर्गि॒र्भिर्वा॑वृ॒धे गृ॑ण॒तामृषी॑णां ॥ -३ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अध्वयो इति । वीर । प्र । महे । सुतानां । इंद्राय । भर । सः । हि । अस्य । राजा।  
> यः । पूर्व्याभिः । उत । नूतनाभिः । गीःभिः । ववृधे । गृणतां । ऋषीणां ॥ १३ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे वीर हविषां विशेषेण प्रेरयितरध्वयो महे महतेऽस्मा इंद्राय
> सुतानां ॥ कर्मणि षष्ठी ॥ सुतानभिषुतान् सोमान् प्र भर । प्रहर । प्रगमय ।
> स हि खल्विंद्रोऽस्य सोमस्य राजा स्वामी भवति । य इंद्रः पूर्व्याभिः पूर्वकाले
> कृताभिरुतापि च नूतनाभिरिदानीं क्रियॆमाणाभिर्गृणतां स्तुवतामृषिणां संबंधिनीभिर्गीर्भिरुभयविधाभिः स्तुतिभिर्वावृथे वर्धते । स इंद्रो यस्मात्सोमस्य राजा
> शस्मादस्मै सोमः प्रदेय इत्यर्थः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O hero, O priest who sends forth the oblations in a special manner: offer the pressed Soma to the great Indra. For that Indra is the lord of this Soma, he who grows strong through the hymns of the praising sages, hymns of both kinds, made in the former time and now being made. Because Indra is the lord of the Soma, Soma is to be given to him; this is the meaning.

### Printed p. 117

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- यः (*yaḥ*)—the Indra who
- पूर्व्याभिः (*pūrvyābhiḥ*)—of the ancient time
- उत (*uta*)—and
- नूतनाभिः (*nūtanābhiḥ*)—of the modern time
- गृणतां (*gṛṇatāṃ*)—praising
- ऋषीणां (*ṛṣīṇāṃ*)—made by the sages
- गीर्भिः (*gīrbhiḥ*)—by hymns
- वावृधे (*vāvṛdhe*)—grows strong
- स हि (*sa hi*)—that same Indra
- अस्य (*asya*)—of this Soma
- राजा (*rājā*)—is the lord (therefore)
- वीर (*vīra*)—offering the oblation
- अध्वर्यो (*adhvaryo*)—O Adhvaryu
- महे (*mahe*)—to the exalted
- इंद्राय (*iṃdrāya*)—to Indra
- सुतानां (*sutānāṃ*)—the pressed Somas
- प्र भर (*pra bhara*)—offer

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

Indra, who grows strong through hymns that are of the ancient time and also of the modern time, made by the praising sages, that same Indra is the lord of this Soma. Therefore, O Adhvaryu who offers the oblation, offer the pressed Somas to the exalted Indra.

##### English translation
Ministrant priests offer libations to the mighty Indra, for he is their king, he who has been exalted by the ancient and recent hymns of adoring sages.

#### Ṛk 14

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अ॒स्य मदे॑ पु॒रु वर्पां॑सि वि॒द्वानिंद्रो॑ वृ॒त्राण्य॑ प्र॒ती ज॑घान ।  
> तमु॒ प्र हो॑षि॒ मधु॑मंतमस्मै॒ सोमं॑ वी॒राय॑ शि॒प्रिणे॒  ॑
> पिबध्यै॥ १४ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अस्य । मदे । पुरु । वर्षांसि । विद्वान् । इंद्रः । वृत्राणि । अप्रति । जघान ॥  
> तं । ऊं इति । प्र । होषि । मधुमंतं । अस्मै । सोमं । वीराय । शिप्रिणे ।  
> पिबध्यै ॥ १४ ॥

### Printed p. 118

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> प्रथमे रात्रिपर्याये मैत्रावरुणस्यास्य मद इति शस्त्रयाज्या । सूत्रितं च ।
> अस्य मदे पुरु वर्षांसि विद्वानिति याज्या । आ. ६-४ । इति ॥
> अस्य सोमस्य पानेन मदे वर्षे सति विद्वानभिज्ञ इंद्रः पुरु पुरूणि बहूनि
> वर्पांस्यावरकाणि वृत्राणि शत्रुवनप्रति स्वयमन्यैरप्रतिगतः सन् जघान । हतवान् ।
> यद्वा । वर्प इति रूपनाम । बहूनि वर्पांस्यसुरमायया निर्मितानि रूपाणि
> विद्वान् जानन्निंद्रो वृत्राण्यावरकाणि रक्षःप्रभृतीनीतः पूर्वमन्यैरप्रतिगतानि
> जघान । हतवान् । मधुमंतं माधुर्यवंतं तमु तमेव सोमं शिप्रिणे
> शोभनहनुकायास्मै वीराय शत्रुवनां वीरयित्र इंद्राय पिबध्यै पातुं
> प्र होषि । हे अध्वयो प्रजुहुधि ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

In the first night-session this verse, beginning अस्य मदे (*asya made*), is the śastra-yājyā of the Maitrāvaruṇa priest. It is also stated in the sūtra: "अस्य मदे पुरु वर्षांसि विद्वानिति याज्या (*asya made puru varṣāṃsi vidvāniti yājyā*)", Ā. 6-4. When the exhilaration arises from drinking this Soma, the knowing Indra, himself unopposed by others, slew many enemies, the obstructors. Or else: वर्प (*varpa*) is a name for form; knowing the many forms created by the magic of the Asuras, Indra slew the obstructors, the Rakṣas and others, that none before had overcome. Offer that same sweet Soma into the fire for this hero, the fair-jawed one, the one who makes the enemies tremble, to drink it. The address is to the Adhvaryu: offer it.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अस्य (*asya*)—by drinking this Soma
- मदे (*made*)—when exhilaration arises
- विद्वान् (*vidvān*)—the wise
- इंद्रः (*iṃdraḥ*)—Indra
- पुरु (*puru*)—those of many number
- वर्षांसि (*varṣāṃsi*)—and of the form of obstructors
- वृत्राणि (*vṛtrāṇi*)—enemies
- अप्रति (*aprati*)—unopposed
- जघान (*jaghāna*)—slew
- शिप्रिणे (*śipriṇe*)—having an excellent jaw
- वीराय (*vīrāya*)—and valiant
- अस्मै (*asmai*)—to this Indra
- मधुमंतं (*madhumaṃtaṃ*)—endowed with sweetness
- तमु (*tamu*)—and famed
- सोमं (*somaṃ*)—that same Soma
- पिबध्यॆ (*pibadhyè*)—to drink
- प्र होषि (*pra hoṣi*)—offer in the form of an oblation

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

When exhilaration arose from drinking this Soma, the wise Indra, unopposed, slew the enemies, who were many in number and of the form of obstructors. To this Indra, who has an excellent jaw and is valiant, offer in the form of an oblation the Soma, which is endowed with sweetness and famed, so that he may drink it.

##### English translation
In the exhilaration of this Soma, the wise Indra, irresistible, has destroyed numerous opposing enemies : offer the sweet-flavoured beverage to that hero. the handsome-chinned, to drink.

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

In the first night-session the priest called Maitrāvaruṇa is to recite this Ṛk beginning अस्य मदे (*asya made*) as the śastra-yājyā mantra; this is explained by the sūtra of the Āśvalāyana-śrauta-sūtra, "अस्य मदे पुरु वर्षांसि विद्वानिति याज्या (*asya made puru varṣāṃsi vidvāniti yājyā*)" (Ā. 6-4).

### Printed p. 119

#### Ṛk 15

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> पाता॑ सु॒तमिंद्रो॑ अस्तु॒ सोमं॒ हंता॑ वृ॒त्रं वज्रे॑ण मंदसा॒नः ।  
> गंता॑ य॒ज्ञं प॑रा॒वत॑श्चि॒दच्छा॒ वसु॑र्धी॒नाम॑वि॒ता का॒रुधा॑याः॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> पाता । सुतं । इंद्रः । अस्तु । सोमं । हंता । वृत्रं । वज्रेण । मंदसानः ।  
> गंता । यज्ञं । परावतः । चित् । अच्छ । वसुः । धीनां । अविता । कारुधायाः ॥१५॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> द्वितीये रात्रिपर्याये मैत्रावरुणस्यैव पाता सुतमिति शस्त्रयाज्या ।
> सूत्रितं च । पाता सुतमिंद्रो अस्तु सोमं हंता वृत्रमिति याज्या । आ. ६-४ । इति ॥
> अयमिंद्रः सुतमभिषुतं सोमं पातास्तु । साधु पाता भवतु ॥ साधुकारिणि
> तृन् । आत एव न लोकाव्ययेति षष्ठिप्रतिषेधात् द्वितीया । एवं हंतेत्यादावपि
> द्रष्टव्यं ॥ तेन च सोमेन मंडसानो माद्यन् वज्रेणायुधेन वृत्रमावरकं
> शत्रुं हंतास्तु । भवतु । एतदुभयार्थं परावतश्चित् दूरदेशादपि यज्ञमच्छास्मदीयं यज्ञमभि गंता भवतु । दूरदेशस्थोऽपि शीघ्रमस्मदृज्ञॆ सोमपानायागच्छत्वित्यर्थः । कीदृश इंद्रः । वसुः सर्वेषां निवासयिता धीनां ध्यात्यणां
> स्तोत्यणां कर्मणां नाविता रक्षिता कारुधायाः कारूणां कर्तृणां यजमानानां
> धारयिता ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

In the second night-session this verse, beginning पाता सुतं (*pātā sutaṃ*), is the śastra-yājyā of the Maitrāvaruṇa priest alone. It is also stated in the sūtra: "पाता सुतमिंद्रो अस्तु सोमं हंता वृत्रमिति याज्या (*pātā sutamiṃdro astu somaṃ haṃtā vṛtramiti yājyā*)", Ā. 6-4. May this Indra be a good drinker of the pressed Soma. And, exhilarated by that Soma, may he be the slayer, with the thunderbolt, his weapon, of Vṛtra, the obstructing enemy. For both purposes, may he come to our sacrifice even from a far-off place; the meaning is: though staying far away, let him come quickly to our sacrifice to drink Soma. What kind of Indra? The one who is the dwelling-giver of all, the protector of the rites of the thinkers and praisers, the upholder of the doers, the sacrificers.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- इंद्रः (*iṃdraḥ*)—Indra
- सुतं (*sutaṃ*)—the pressed
- सोमं (*somaṃ*)—Soma
- पाता अस्तु (*pātā astu*)—let him be the drinker
- मंदसानः (*maṃdasānaḥ*)—satisfied by it
- वज्रेण (*vajreṇa*)—with the thunderbolt
- वृत्रं (*vṛtraṃ*)—the enemy
- हंता (*haṃtā*)—let him be the destroyer
- वसुः (*vasuḥ*)—the dweller-giver of all
- धीनां (*dhīnāṃ*)—of the praisers
- अविता (*avitā*)—the protector
- कारुधायाः (*kārudhāyāḥ*)—and the upholder of the sacrificers, Indra
- परावतश्चित् (*parāvataścit*)—even though in a very distant place
- यज्ञं अच्छ (*yajñaṃ accha*)—facing our sacrifice
- गंता (*gaṃtā*)—let him be the one who comes,

### Printed p. 120

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

Let Indra be the drinker of the pressed Soma. Satisfied by it, let him be the destroyer of the enemy with the thunderbolt. Let Indra, the dweller-giver of all, the protector of the praisers and the upholder of the sacrificers, be one who comes facing our sacrifice, even though he is in a very distant place.

##### English translation
May Indra be the drinker of this effused Soma juice, and exhilarated by it, become the destroyer of Vritra by the thunderbolt: may he come, although from afar, to our sacrifice, (he who is) the giver of dwellings, the upholder of the celebrator (of religious rites).

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

In the second night-session the priest called Maitrāvaruṇa is to recite this Ṛk beginning पाता सुतं (*pātā sutaṃ*) as the śastra-yājyā mantra; this is explained by the sūtra of the Āśvalāyana-śrauta-sūtra, "पाता सुतमिंद्रो अस्तु सोमं हंता वृत्रमिति याज्या (*pātā sutamiṃdro astu somaṃ haṃtā vṛtramiti yājyā*)" (Ā. 6-4).

#### Ṛk 16

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> इ॒दं त्यत्पात्र॑मिंद्र॒पान॒मिंद्र॑स्य प्रि॒यम॒मृत॑मपायि ।  
> मत्स॒द्यथा॑ सौमन॒साय॑ दे॒वं व्य१॒॑गस्मद्वेषो॑ यु॒यव॒द्व्यंहः॑ ॥ १६ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> इदं । त्यत् । पात्रं । इंद्रपानं । इंद्रस्य । प्रियं । अमृतं । अपायि ।  
> मत्सत् । यथा । सौमनसाय । देवं । वि । अस्मत् । द्वेषः । युयवत् । वि । अंहः ॥१६॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> तृतीये रात्रिपर्याये प्रशास्तुरिदं त्यदिति शस्त्रयाज्या । सूत्रितं च । इदं
> त्यत्पात्रमिंद्रपानमिति याज्या । आ. ६-४ । इति ॥
> पात्रं पातव्यमिंद्रपानमिंद्रस्य पानार्हमिंद्रस्य प्रियमनुकूलं त्यत्तदिदं
> सोमात्मकममृतमपायि । इंद्रः पिबतु ॥ व्यत्ययेन कर्तरि चिन् ॥ यद्वा ।
> एतदमृतमपायि । इंद्रेण पीयतां । यथा येन प्रकारेण पीतः सोमः

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

In the third night-session this verse, beginning इदं त्यत् (*idaṃ tyat*), is the śastra-yājyā of the Praśāstṛ priest. It is also stated in the sūtra: "इदं त्यत्पात्रमिंद्रपानमिति याज्या (*idaṃ tyatpātramiṃdrapānamiti yājyā*)", Ā. 6-4. The vessel, to be drunk, fit for Indra's drinking, dear and favourable to Indra, this Soma-nature immortal drink, has been drunk; let Indra drink. The passive form stands for the active by a change of voice. Or else: let this immortal drink be drunk by Indra, in such a manner that the Soma is drunk.

### Printed p. 121

> सौमनसाय सुमनस्त्वाय देवमिंद्रं मत्सत् मादयेत्तथा पीयतामित्यर्थः ।
> स पीतसोम इंद्रो द्वेषो द्वेष्ट नस्मदस्मत्तो वि युयवत् । वियोजयतु ।
> अंहः पापं चास्मत्तो वियोजयतु ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

सौमनसाय (*saumanasāya*) means for the sake of good-mindedness; Soma is to be drunk in such a manner that it delights the god Indra. Having drunk the Soma, Indra is to remove from us hatred, that is, the hater, and sin.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- यथा (*yathā*)—in whatever manner
- सौमनसाय (*saumanasāya*)—for the sake of showing favour
- देवं (*devaṃ*)—Indra
- मत्सत् (*matsat*)—for the very reason that it gladdens
- पात्रं (*pātraṃ*)—that which is fit to be drunk
- इंद्रपानं (*iṃdrapānaṃ*)—that which is fit for Indra to drink
- इंद्रस्य (*iṃdrasya*)—to Indra
- प्रियं (*priyaṃ*)—agreeable
- त्यत् (*tyat*)—famed
- इदं (*idaṃ*)—of the nature of Soma
- अमृतं (*amṛtaṃ*)—this ambrosia
- अपायि (*apāyi*)—let it be drunk (after that, Indra)
- द्वेषः (*dveṣaḥ*)—the enemies
- अस्मत् (*asmat*)—from us
- वि युयवत् (*vi yuyavat*)—let him keep far away and separate
- अंहः (*aṃhaḥ*)—sin
- वि (*vi*)—let him keep far away

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

In order that Indra may attain the delight of showing us favour, let this ambrosia be drunk, which is fit to be drunk, fit for Indra to drink, agreeable to Indra, famed, and of the nature of Soma. Thereafter let Indra keep our enemies and our sins apart from us (or let him destroy them).

##### English translation
May this ambrosia, the appropriate beverage of Indra, of which he is fond, be quaffed (by him), so that it may inspire the divinity with favourable feelings (towards us), and that he may remove from us our adversaries, and (all) iniquity.

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

In the third night-session the priest called Praśāstra **NOTE** is to recite this Ṛk beginning इदं त्यत् (*idaṃ tyat*) as the śastra-yājyā mantra; this is explained by the sūtra of the Āśvalāyana-śrauta-sūtra, "इदं त्यत्पात्रमिंद्रपानमिति याज्या (*idaṃ tyatpātramiṃdrapānamiti yājyā*)" (Ā. 6-4).

#### Ṛk 17

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> ए॒ना मं॑दा॒नो ज॒हि शू॑र॒ शत्रू॑ञ्जा॒ मिमजा॑मिं मघवन्न॒मित्रा॑न् ।  
> अ॒भि॒षे॒णा अ॒भ्या॒३॒॑सि देदि॑शाना॒न्परा॑च इंद्र॒ प्र मृ॑णा ज॒ही च॑॥१७॥

### Printed p. 122

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> एना । मंदानः । जहि । शूर । शत्रून् । जामिं । अजामिं । मघवन् । मित्रान् ।  
> अभिऽसेनान् ! अभि । आदेदिशानान् । पराचः । इंद्र । प्र । मृण । जहि । च ॥ १७ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे मघवन् धनवन् शूर शौर्यवन्निंद्र एनैनेन प्रकृतेनानेन सोमेन
> मंदानो मंदमानो माद्यन् जामिं ज्ञातिमजामिं तद्विलक्षणमेवमुभयविधानमित्रानस्मत्पतिकूलाचरणपरान् शत्रुन् जहि । हिंसितान्कुरु । किंचाभिषेणान्
> अस्मान्नृत्यभिगताः सेना येषां तादृशानस्मदभिमुखमादेदिशानानायुधानि पुनः
> पुनरादिशतो विसृजतः शत्रुंश्च हे इंद्र पराचः प्र मृण । ते यथा पराज्मुखा
> भवंति तथा बाधस्व । तदनंतरं तान् जहि च ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Maghavan, wealthy one, O hero, valiant Indra, exhilarated by this Soma now at hand, slay the enemies, whether जामिं (*jāmiṃ*), a kinsman, or अजामिं (*ajāmiṃ*), one unlike him; that is, enemies of both kinds, intent on acting against us. Do them harm. Moreover, O Indra, drive back the enemies who are अभिषेणान् (*abhiṣeṇān*), whose armies advance against us, and who आदेदिशानान् (*ādediśānān*) again and again aim and hurl their weapons in our direction. Press them so that they turn their faces away; after that, slay them.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- मघवन् (*maghavan*)—wealthy
- शूर (*śūra*)—and valiant, O Indra
- एना (*enā*)—by this Soma
- मंदानः (*maṃdānaḥ*)—having become glad
- जामिं (*jāmiṃ*)—whether a kinsman
- अजामिं (*ajāmiṃ*)—or not a kinsman, such
- अमित्रान् (*amitrān*)—those who do us harm
- शत्रुन् (*śatrun*)—enemies
- जहि (*jahi*)—destroy
- इंद्र (*iṃdra*)—O Indra
- अभिषेणान् (*abhiṣeṇān*)—those who fall upon us
- अभि आदेदिशानान् (*abhi ādediśānān*)—and those who hurl weapons at us, the enemies
- पराचः (*parācaḥ*)—so that they run back
- प्र मृण (*pra mṛṇa*)—do them harm
- जहि च (*jahi ca*)—and destroy them also

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, wealthy and valiant, gladdened by this Soma, destroy our enemies. Whether that enemy be a kinsman to us or not a kinsman, when he falls upon us and hurls weapons at us, do harm to such enemies so that they run back, and destroy them.

##### English translation
Exhilarated by it, valiant Maghavan, slay our unfriendly adversaries, whether kinsmen or unrelated (to us) : put to flight, Indra, hostile armies menacing us (with their weapons), and slay them.

#### Ṛk 18

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> आ॒सु ष्मा॑ णो मघवन्निंद्र पृ॒त्स्व१॒॑ १ स्मभ्यं॒ महि॒ वरि॑वः ॒ ॑
> सुगं कः ।  
> अ॒पां तो॒कस्य॒ तन॑यस्य जे॒ष इंद्र॑ सू॒रीन्कृ॑णु॒हि स्मा॑ नो  ॒
> अर्धं ॥ १८ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> असु । स्म । नः । मघवन् । इंद्र । पृत्सु । अस्मभ्यं । महि । वरिवः । सुगं ।  
> करिति कः ।  
> अपां । तोकस्य । तनयस्य । जेषे इंद्र । सूरीन् । कृणुहि स्म । नः । अर्धं ॥१८॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे मघवन् धनवन्निंद्र नोऽस्मदीयास्वासु पृत्सु पृतनासु संग्रामेष्वस्मभ्यं महि महत्सुगं सुखेन प्राप्यं वरिवो धनं कः । कुरु । स्मेति पूरणः । अपि
> चि हे इंद्र अपामाप्तव्यानां धनानां वोदकानां ना तोकस्य पुत्रस्य तनयस्य
> पौत्रस्य च जेषे जयार्थं सूरीन् स्तोत्यन् नोऽस्मानर्धमर्धान् समृद्धान्
> कृणुहि । कुरु । यद्वा । अर्धकारिणः शत्रूणां खंडयित्यन् कुरु । स्मेति पूरकः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Maghavan, wealthy Indra, in these battles of ours make for us great wealth, वरिवः (*varivaḥ*), that is easily obtained; स्म (*sma*) is a filler. Moreover, O Indra, for the winning of waters, or of wealth that is to be gained **NOTE**, of a son and of a grandson, make us, the praisers, अर्धं (*ardhaṃ*), that is, prosperous. Or else: make us the cutters-down of the enemies who are अर्धकारिणः (*ardhakāriṇaḥ*); स्म (*sma*) is the filler.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- मघवन् (*maghavan*)—wealthy
- इंद्र (*iṃdra*)—O Indra
- नः (*naḥ*)—belonging to us
- आसु पृत्सु (*āsu pṛtsu*)—in these battles
- अस्मभ्यं (*asmabhyaṃ*)—for us
- महि (*mahi*)—great
- सुगं (*sugaṃ*)—and easily obtained
- वरिवः (*varivaḥ*)—wealth
- कः (*kaḥ*)—bring about
- इंद्र (*iṃdra*)—O Indra
- अपां (*apāṃ*)—for the sake of water
- तोकस्य (*tokasya*)—for the sake of a son
- तनयस्य (*tanayasya*)—for the sake of a grandson
- जेषे (*jeṣe*)—to gain victory
- सूरीन् (*sūrīn*)—who are praisers
- नः (*naḥ*)—us
- अर्थं (*arthaṃ*)—as prosperous ones
- कृणुहि (*kṛṇuhi*)—make

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O wealthy Indra, in these battles that have put us into distress, bring about for us wealth that is great and easily obtained. O Indra, to gain victory for the sake of water, for the sake of a son and for the sake of a grandson, make us, the praisers, prosperous.

### Printed p. 124

##### English translation
Affluent Indra, facilitate to us (the acquirement of) vast riches in these our battles: (enable us) to ga n the victory: make us prosperous with rain, and with sons and graudsons.

#### Ṛk 19

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> आ त्वा॒ हर॑यो॒ वृष॑णो युजा॒ना वृष॑रथासो॒ वृष॑रश्म॒योऽत्याः॑ ।  
> अ॒स्म॒त्रांचो॒ वृष॑णो वज्र॒वाहो॒ वृष्णे॒ मदा॑य सु॒युजो॑  
> वहंतु ॥ १९ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> आ । त्वा । हरयः । वृषणः । युजानाः । वृषरथासः । वृषरश्मयः । अत्याः ।  
> अस्मत्रांच । वृषणः । वज्रवाहः । वृष्णे । मदाय । सुयुजः । वहंतु ॥ १९ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे इंद्र त्वा त्वां हरयोऽश्वा वृष्णे वर्षित्रे मदाय मदकराय
> सोमायेदृशं सोमं पाययितुमा वहंतु । कीदृशा अश्वाः । वृषणः कामानां
> वर्षितारो युजानाः स्वयमेव रथे युज्यमाना वृषरथासः । वृषा कामाभिवर्षको रथो येषां ते तथोक्ताः । वृषरश्मयः । वर्षितारो रत्नयः प्रग्रहा
> येषां तादृशाः । अत्याः सततगामिनोऽस्मत्रांचोऽस्मान्वृत्यंचंतो गच्छंतो
> वृषणो नित्यतरुणा वज्रवाहो वज्रादीन्यायुधानि वहंतः सुयुजः शोभनयोजनाः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Indra, let the horses bring you to drink the Soma, which is the showerer's, that is, gladdening, exhilarating. What kind of horses? They are वृषणः (*vṛṣaṇaḥ*), showerers of desires; युजानाः (*yujānāḥ*), yoked of themselves to the chariot; वृषरथासः (*vṛṣarathāsaḥ*), those whose chariot is a showerer, one that rains desires; वृषरश्मयः (*vṛṣaraśmayaḥ*), those whose reins are showerers, that is, whose bridles rain; अत्याः (*atyāḥ*), ever moving; अस्मत्रांचः (*asmatrāṃcaḥ*), going towards us; वृषणः (*vṛṣaṇaḥ*), ever young; वज्रवाहः (*vajravāhaḥ*), bearing weapons such as the thunderbolt; सुयुजः (*suyujaḥ*), well yoked.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- (O Indra) वृषणः (*vṛṣaṇaḥ*)—showerers of desires
- युजानाः (*yujānāḥ*)—joined to the chariot
- वृषरथासः (*vṛṣarathāsaḥ*)—possessing a chariot that bestows what is wished
- वृषरश्मयः (*vṛṣaraśmayaḥ*)—possessing bridles that cause rain
- अत्याः (*atyāḥ*)—swift-going
- अस्मत्रांचः (*asmatrāṃcaḥ*)—coming towards us

### Printed p. 125

- वृषणः (*vṛṣaṇaḥ*)—possessing youth
- वज्रवाहः (*vajravāhaḥ*)—bearing weapons such as the thunderbolt
- सुयुजः (*suyujaḥ*)—excellently yoked
- हरयः (*harayaḥ*)—the horses
- त्वा (*tvā*)—you
- वृष्णे (*vṛṣṇe*)—which is rich in wealth
- मदाय (*madāya*)—and gladdening, for the drinking of Soma
- आ वहंतु (*ā vahaṃtu*)—let them bring

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, let horses that are showerers of desires, joined to the chariot, possessing a chariot that bestows what is wished, swift-going, coming towards us, possessing youth, bearing weapons such as the thunderbolt, and excellently yoked, bring you for the drinking of Soma, which is rich in wealth and gladdening.

##### English translation
Let your vigorous steeds, harnessed of their own will, drawing your wish-bestowing chariot, guided by shower-yielding reins, quick-moving, hastening towards us, youthful, thunder-bearing, well-yoked, bring you to the bountiful, exhilarating (libations).

#### Ṛk 20

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> आ ते॑ वृष॒न्वृष॑णो॒ द्रोण॑मस्थुर्घृत॒पुषो॒ नोर्मयो॒  ॑
> मदंतः ।  
> इंद्र॒ प्र तुभ्यं॒ वृष॑भिः सु॒तानां॒ वृष्णे॑ भरंति वृष॒भाय॒  ॑
> सोमं ॥ २० ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> आ । ते । वृषन् । वृषणः । द्रोणं । अस्तुः । घृतपुषः । न । ऊर्मयः । मदंतः ।  
> इंद्र । प्र । तुभ्यं । वृषभिः । सुतानां । वृष्णे । भरंति । वृषभाय । सोमं ॥ २० ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे वृषन् कामानां वर्षितरिंद्र वृषणः सेक्तारो युवानस्त्वदीया अश्वा
> घृतपुषो नोर्मयो धृतमुदकं सिंचंतः सामुद्रास्तरंगा इव मदंतो

### Printed p. 126

> हृष्यंतो मत्ताः संतस्ते त्वदीयं द्रोणं द्रुममयं रथमास्थुः । अतिष्ठंत ।
> अतस्त्वमागच्छेति भावः । कुत इत्यत आह । हे इंद्र वृष्णे यूने नित्यतरुणाय
> वृषभाय कामानां वर्षयित्रे तुभ्यं वृषभिः कामाभिवर्षकैग्रावभिः सुतानामभिषुतानां सोमानां मध्यॆ त्वद्भागरूपं सोमं यस्मादध्वर्यवः प्र भरंति
> प्रहरंति होमार्थं प्रणयंति तस्मादागच्छेत्यर्थः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Indra, showerer of desires, your young horses, वृषणः (*vṛṣaṇaḥ*), sprinklers, pouring ghee, that is, water, like waves of the ocean, exult and become glad; they have mounted your द्रोणं (*droṇaṃ*), your chariot made of wood. Therefore, the sense is, come. Why? He says: O Indra, for you, the young, ever youthful bull, the showerer of desires, the Adhvaryus offer forward, carry forward for the oblation, the Soma that is your portion, among the Somas pressed by the showering stones. Therefore come; this is the meaning.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- वृषन् (*vṛṣan*)—O Indra, showerer of desires
- वृषणः (*vṛṣaṇaḥ*)—possessing youth
- धृतपुषः (*dhṛtapuṣaḥ*)—pouring forth water
- ऊर्मयो न (*ūrmayo na*)—like the waves of the sea that dance
- मदंतः (*madaṃtaḥ*)—showing gladness, your horses
- ते (*te*)—your
- द्रोणं (*droṇaṃ*)—chariot
- आ अस्तुः (*ā astuḥ*)—stand joined to
- इंद्र (*iṃdra*)—O Indra
- वृष्णी (*vṛṣṇī*)—to you, young
- वृषभाय (*vṛṣabhāya*)—and showerer of desires
- तुभ्यं (*tubhyaṃ*)—for you
- वृषभिः (*vṛṣabhiḥ*)—with the showering stones (grāvan)
- सुतानां (*sutānāṃ*)—among the pressed Somas
- सोमं (*somaṃ*)—the juice allotted to your portion
- प्र भरंति (*pra bharaṃti*)—they offer in the form of oblation

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, showerer of desires, your horses, which are young, pouring forth water and showing gladness like the dancing waves of the sea, stand joined to your chariot. O Indra, for you, who are young and a showerer of desires, the Adhvaryus offer in the form of oblation the juice that belongs to your portion among the Soma juices pressed by crushing with stones.

##### English translation
Showerer (of benefits), your vigorous water-shedding steeds, like the waves (of the sea), exulting, are harnessed to your car ; for they, (the priests , offer to you, the showerer (of benefits), ever youthful, the libation of the Soma juices expressed by the stones.

#### Ṛk 21

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> वृषा॑सि दि॒वो वृ॑ष॒भः पृ॑थि॒व्या वृषा॒ सिंधू॑नां वृष॒भः  ॑
> स्तियानां ।  
> वृष्णे॑ त॒ इंदु॑र्वृषभ पीपाय स्वा॒दू रसो॑ मधु॒पेयो॒  ॑
> वराय ॥ २१ ॥

### Printed p. 127

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> वृषा । आसि । दिवः । वृषभः । पृथिव्याः । वृषा । सिंधूनां । वृषभः । स्तियानां ।  
> वृष्णे । ते । इंदुः । वृषभ । पीपाय । स्वादुः । रसः । मधुपेयः । वराय ॥ २१ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे इंद्र त्वं दिवो द्युलोकस्य वृषासि । हविर्भिः सेक्ता भवसि । पृथिव्या
> भूमेश्च वृषभः कामानां वर्षिता । सिंधूनां स्यंदनशीलानां च नदीनां वृषा
> वर्षेण पूरयितासि । तथा स्तियानां संघीभूतानां स्वावरजंगमात्मनां प्राणिनां
> वृषभः कामानां वर्षिता भवसि । यद्वा । स्तियाः संघीभूता आपः । तासां
> वर्षितासि । वृषभ कामानां वर्षकेंद्र वराय श्रेष्ठाय वृष्णे सेक्त्रे ते तुभ्यं
> त्वदर्थं स्वादुर्मधुरो रसो रसनीयः प्रशस्यो रसवान्वा रसात्मको ना
> मधुपेयो मधुवत्पातव्य इंदुः सोमः पीपाय । प्यायते । वर्धते ।
> आतस्त्वमागत्य तं सोमं पिबेति भावः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Indra, you are the showerer of heaven, दिवः (*divaḥ*): you sprinkle it with oblations. You are the वृषभः (*vṛṣabhaḥ*) of the earth, the showerer of desires. You are the वृषा (*vṛṣā*) of the rivers, flowing ones, filling them with rain. Likewise you are the showerer of desires of the स्तियानां (*stiyānāṃ*), the beings gathered together, of the nature of the stationary and the moving. Or else: स्तियाः (*stiyāḥ*) are the waters gathered together, and you are the one who showers them. O showerer of desires, Indra, for you, the excellent one, the sprinkler, the sweet juice, flavourful, or praiseworthy, or full of juice, of the nature of juice, to be drunk like honey, इंदुः (*iṃduḥ*), the Soma, swells and grows; therefore, the sense is, come and drink that Soma.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- वृषभ (*vṛṣabha*)—O Indra, showerer of desires
- (त्वं (*tvaṃ*)—you)
- दिवः (*divaḥ*)—for the heavenly world
- वृषा (*vṛṣā*)—as the bestower of what is wished
- असि (*asi*)—you are
- पृथिव्या (*pṛthivyā*)—for the earth
- वृषभः (*vṛṣabhaḥ*)—you are the showerer of desires
- सिंधूनां (*siṃdhūnāṃ*)—for the rivers
- वृषा (*vṛṣā*)—you fill the water with rain
- स्तियानां (*stiyānāṃ*)—for all beings that form a collection
- वृषभः (*vṛṣabhaḥ*)—you are the showerer of desires
- वृषभ (*vṛṣabha*)—O Indra, bestower of what is wished
- वराय (*varāya*)—for you, the excellent
- वृष्णे (*vṛṣṇe*)—and the cause of rain
- ते (*te*)—for you
- स्वादुः (*svāduḥ*)—sweet
- रसः (*rasaḥ*)—agreeable (of the nature of juice)
- मधुपेयः (*madhupeyaḥ*)—to be drunk like honey
- इंदुः (*iṃduḥ*)—the Soma
- पीपाय (*pīpāya*)—is ready for drinking

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, showerer of desires, you are the bestower of what is wished for the heavenly world. For the earth you are the showerer of desires by giving rain. You fill the rivers with water by rain. For all beings that form a collection you are the showerer of desires. O Indra, bestower of what is wished, for you, the excellent and the cause of rain, the Soma juice is ready for drinking.

##### English translation
You, Indra, are the showerer of heaven, the bedewer of earth, the the feeder of the rivers, the supplier of the aggregated (waters): for you,

### Printed p. 128

showerer (of desires), who are the most excellent shedder of rain, the sweet Soma, the honey-flavoured juice, is ready to be quaffed.

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

On the word स्तियानां (*stiyānāṃ*) Yāska has given this explanation: स्तिया आपो भवंति स्त्यायनात् । वृषा सिंधूनां वृषभः स्तियानां इत्यपि निगमो भवति ॥ (*stiyā āpo bhavaṃti styāyanāt | vṛṣā siṃdhūnāṃ vṛṣabhaḥ stiyānāṃ ityapi nigamo bhavati ‖*) (Ni. 6-17). स्तियाः (*stiyāḥ*) means water, because the word स्त्यायन (*styāyana*) has the meaning "bring together". When the earth, and the many substances in it, are broken into fine particles and do not cohere, water makes them come together (by pouring water and kneading). Water has the power of making two substances unite. Therefore water has received the name स्तिया (*stiyā*). In this matter the Vedic sentence in the present Ṛk, वृषा सिंधूनां वृषभः स्तियानां (*vṛṣā siṃdhūnāṃ vṛṣabhaḥ stiyānāṃ*), is itself the example; this is the purport of the etymology.

#### Ṛk 22

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अ॒यं दे॒वः सह॑सा॒ जाय॑मान॒ इंद्रे॑ण यु॒जा प॒णिम॑स्तभायत् ।  
> अ॒यं स्वस्य॑ पि॒तुरायु॑धा॒नींदु॑रमुष्णा॒दशि॑वस्य मा॒याः ॥२२॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अयं । देवः । सहसा । जायमानः । इंद्रेण । युजा । पणिं । अस्तभायत् ।  
> अयं । स्वस्य । पितुः । आयुधानि । इंदुः । अमुष्णात् । अशिवस्य । मायाः ॥ २२ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> देवो द्योतमानोऽयमिंदुः सोम इंद्रेण युजा सख्या सह
> जायमानः प्रादुर्भवन् षणिं वणिजं गवामादातारं वलाख्यमसुरं । यद्वा ।
> पणीनां स्वामित्वात्पणिरित्युच्यते । तमसुरं सहसा बलेनास्तभायत् । अस्तभ्नात् ।
> न्यरुधत् । अपि च स्वस्य धनस्य गोरूपस्य पितुः पालयितुरशिवस्यासुखकरस्य

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

This shining Indu, the Soma, born together with Indra his friend, the yoked one, stopped by his strength the Paṇi, the merchant, the Asura named Vala, who seizes cattle. Or else: he is called Paṇi because he is the lord of the Paṇis. He held that Asura back by strength, restrained him. Moreover, of his own wealth in the form of cattle, the protector, the guardian, of the inauspicious, the one who gives no happiness

### Printed p. 129

> वलस्यायुधानि वज्रादीन्यमुष्णात् । अपाहरत् । तदीया मायाश्चामुष्णात् ।
> हे इंद्र त्वत्सख्यबलादेतत्सर्वं सोमः कृतवान् आतस्तव महिमा केन वर्णयितुं
> शक्यत इतींद्रस्तुतिः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The Soma carried off the weapons of Vala, such as the thunderbolt, and carried off his magical powers too. O Indra, by the strength of friendship with you Soma has done all this; who can describe your greatness? This is a praise of Indra.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- देवः (*devaḥ*)—of divine nature
- आयं इंदुः (*āyaṃ iṃduḥ*)—this Soma
- इंद्रेण युजा (*iṃdreṇa yujā*)—joined with Indra
- जायमानः (*jāyamānaḥ*)—as soon as he was born
- पणिं (*paṇiṃ*)—the Asura called Paṇi
- सहसा (*sahasā*)—by his own strength
- अस्तभायत् (*astabhāyat*)—destroyed **NOTE**
- स्वस्य (*svasya*)—of his own wealth in the form of cattle
- पितुः (*pituḥ*)—the guardian
- आशिवस्य (*āśivasya*)—and the inauspicious Vala
- आयुधानि (*āyudhāni*)—the weapons
- अमुष्णात् (*amuṣṇāt*)—he carried off
- मायाः (*māyāḥ*)—the deceitful powers also (he carried off)

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

This divine Soma, joined with Indra, as soon as he was born destroyed by his own strength the Asura called Paṇi. He carried off the weapons of Vala, the guardian of his own wealth in the form of cattle and the inauspicious one. He carried off his deceitful powers also.

##### English translation
This divine Soma, with Indra for its ally, crushed, as soon as generated, Pani by force: this Soma baffled the devices and the weapons of the malignant secreter of (the stolen wealth, the cattle).

#### Ṛk 23

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अ॒यम॑कृणोदु॒षसः॑ सु॒पत्नी॑र॒यं सूर्ये॑ अदधा॒ज्योति॑रं॒तः ।  
> अ॒यं त्रि॒धातु॑ दि॒वि रो॑च॒नेषु॑ त्रि॒तेषु॑ विंदद॒मृतं॒ निगू॑ळ्हं ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अयं । अकृणोत् । उषसः । सुपत्नीः । अयं । सूर्ये । अदधात् । ज्योतिः ।  
> अंतरिति ।  
> अयं । त्रिधातु । दिवि । रोचनेषु । त्रितेषु । विंदत् । अमृतं । निगूळ्हं ॥ २३ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

### Printed p. 130

> आयं सोम उषस उषःकालान् सुपत्नीः शोभनः पतिः पालकः सूर्यो
> यासामुषसां तादृशीरकृणोत् । अकरोत् । आयमेव सूर्ये सूर्यमंडलेंतर्मध्यॆ ज्योतिस्तेजोऽदधात् । निहितवान् । त्रिधातु सवनत्रयरूपेण
> त्रिप्रकारमयं लतारूपः सोमो दिवि द्युलोके रोचनेषु रोचमानेषु
> त्रितेषु तृतीये स्थाने ततेषु विस्तृतेषु देवेषु निगूढं नितरां गूढमदृश्यतॆया
> वर्तमानममृतममृतत्वकारणं पीयूषं विंदत् । अविंदत् । अलभत । हे इंद्र
> त्वत्साहाय्यादेवमेवं सोमः कृतवानितींद्रस्य स्तुतिरधिगंतव्या । यद्वा । इदं
> शब्देनेंद्र एव परामृश्यते । आयमिंद्र उषसः शोभनपतिका आकरोत् । आयमेव
> सूर्येऽंतर्ज्योतिरदधात् । दिवि द्युलोके रोचमानेषु त्रिस्थानगतेषु देवेषु
> मध्येयमेवेंद्रस्त्रिधातु सवनत्रयात्मना त्रिप्रकारं निगूढं पात्रैः संवृतममृतममरणहेतुभूतं सोममविंदत् । अलभत ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

This Soma made the dawns, the times of dawn, सुपत्नीः (*supatnīḥ*), women whose husband and protector is the fair Sūrya; he made them so. He himself placed light, brilliance, within the sun, in the middle of the solar orb. This Soma, in the form of the plant and threefold through the three pressings, found the ambrosia, the immortal drink, the cause of immortality, that lay deeply hidden, unseen, among the gods who shine in heaven and are spread out in the third region. O Indra, the praise of Indra is to be understood thus: Soma has done so by virtue of your help. Or else the word इदं (*idaṃ*) refers to Indra himself: he made the dawns have a fair husband; he placed light within the sun; among the gods shining in heaven, in the three places, this Indra found the Soma, threefold through the three pressings, hidden, covered by the vessels, the cause of deathlessness.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- आयं (*āyaṃ*)—this Soma
- उषसः (*uṣasaḥ*)—the dawns
- सुपत्नीः (*supatnīḥ*)—as wives of the excellent Sūrya
- अकृणोत् (*akṛṇot*)—made
- आयं (*āyaṃ*)—this same Soma
- सूर्ये (*sūrye*)—of the solar orb
- अंतः (*aṃtaḥ*)—in the middle part
- ज्योतिः (*jyotiḥ*)—brilliance
- अदधात् (*adadhāt*)—placed
- अयं (*ayaṃ*)—this Soma in the form of the plant
- दिवि (*divi*)—in the heavenly world
- रोचनेषु (*rocaneṣu*)—shining
- त्रितेषु (*triteṣu*)—and spread out in the third place, among the gods
- निगूळ्हं (*nigūḷhaṃ*)—hidden
- त्रिधातु (*tridhātu*)—of three forms in the form of the three pressings
- अमृतं (*amṛtaṃ*)—and free from death, the juice
- विंदत् (*viṃdat*)—obtained

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

This Soma made the dawns to be wives of the excellent Sūrya. This same Soma placed brilliance in the middle of the solar orb. This Soma in the form of the plant obtained the juice, which was hidden among the gods shining in the heavenly world and spread out in the third place, which has three forms in the form of the three pressings, and which is free from death.

##### English translation
This Soma made the dawns happily wedded to the sun : this Soma placed the light within the solar orb: this (Soma) has found the threefold ambrosia hidden in heaven in the three bright regions.

### Printed p. 131

#### Ṛk 24

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अ॒यं द्यावा॑पृथि॒वी वि ष्क॑भायद॒यं रथ॑मयुनक्स्॒यप्तर॑श्मिं ।  
> अ॒यं गोषु॒ शच्या॑ प॒क्वमं॒तः सोमो॑ दाधार॒ दश॑यंत्र॒मुत्सं॑ ॥ २४ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अयं । द्यावापृथिवी इति । वि । स्वभायत् । अयं । रथं । आयुनक् । सप्तरश्मिं ।  
> अयं । गोषु । शच्या । पक्वं । अंतरिति । सोमः । दाधार । दशयंत्रं । उत्सं ॥२४॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> आयं सोमो द्यावापृथिवी दिवं च पृथिवीं च वि ष्कभायत् । विविधमस्थापयत् । अपि चायं सोमः सूर्यस्य च रथं सप्तरश्मिं सप्त रश्मिभिः किरणैरुपेतं
> सप्ताश्वं सप्तचक्रं नवायुनक् । अयोजयत् । तथायं सोमो गोष्टंतर्मध्यॆ
> पक्वं निष्पन्नं शच्या प्रज्ञया संकल्परूपयोत्समुत्सरणशीलं परो दाधार ।
> धारयति । दशयंत्रं । उत्सस्य हेतुगर्भविशेषणमेतत् । उत्सरणहेतुभूतैर्दशभिश्चक्षुःश्रोत्रादिभिर्यंत्रैरुपाय्कॆर्युक्तं । इदं च पदं खैलिकेन मंत्रांतरेण
> व्याख्यायते । चक्षुश्च श्रोत्रं च मनश्च वाक्च प्राणापानौ देह इदं शरीरं ।
> द्वौ प्रत्यंचावनुलोमौ विसर्गावेतं तं मन्ये दशयंत्रमुत्समिति । यद्वा ।
> अयमिंद्रो द्यावापृथिवी वि ष्कभायत् । स्वे स्वे स्थाने स्थिते अकरोत् । अयमेव
> सप्तरश्मिं सूर्यस्य रथमयनक् । आयमेवेंद्रो गोष्टंतः शच्यात्मीयेन
> कर्मणा पक्वं पयो धारयति । एवंभूतेंद्रार्थं सोमो दशयंत्रं
> दशसंख्याकैरैंद्रवायवादिभिर्ग्रह्यैर्यंत्रितैर्गृहीतैरुपेतमुत्समुत्सरणशीलं रसं
> दाधार । धारयति । प्रातःसवन ऐंद्रवायवादयो दश ग्रहा गृह्यंते । तथा च
> ब्राह्मणं । नव प्रातर्ग्रहा गृह्यंते नवभिर्बहिष्पवमाने स्तुवते स्तुते स्तोमे
> दशमं गृह्णाति । ऐ. ब्रा. ३-१ । इति । अन्यत्राम्नातं । दशैतानध्वर्युः प्रातःसवने
> ग्रहान् गृह्णातीति । तदभिप्रायेणेदं दशयंत्रमिति विशेषणं ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

This Soma propped apart heaven and earth, set them each in its place. Moreover, this Soma yoked the chariot of the sun, सप्तरश्मिं (*saptaraśmiṃ*), furnished with seven rays, with seven horses, with seven wheels **NOTE**. So also this Soma holds within the cows, शच्या (*śacyā*), by his intelligence in the form of resolve, the matured, produced milk, a spring, उत्सं (*utsaṃ*), which flows forth. दशयंत्रं (*daśayaṃtraṃ*) is an epithet of the spring: furnished with ten instruments, the eye, ear and the rest, which are the cause of its flowing forth. This word is explained by another khila mantra: चक्षुश्च श्रोत्रं च मनश्च वाक्च प्राणापानौ देह इदं शरीरं । द्वौ प्रत्यंचावनुलोमौ विसर्गावेतं तं मन्ये दशयंत्रमुत्समिति । (*cakṣuśca śrotraṃ ca manaśca vākca prāṇāpānau deha idaṃ śarīraṃ | dvau pratyaṃcāvanulomau visargāvetaṃ taṃ manye daśayaṃtramutsamiti |*). Or else: this Indra propped apart heaven and earth and set them each in its place; he himself yoked the seven-rayed chariot of the sun; this same Indra holds within the cows, by his own act, the matured milk. For such an Indra, Soma holds the spring-like juice, the flowing one, furnished with ten cups, the Aindravāyava and others, drawn and held in check. At the morning pressing ten cups, the Aindravāyava and the rest, are drawn. And so the Brāhmaṇa says: नव प्रातर्ग्रहा गृह्यंते नवभिर्बहिष्पवमाने स्तुवते स्तुते स्तोमे दशमं गृह्णाति । (*nava prātargrahā gṛhyaṃte navabhirbahiṣpavamāne stuvate stute stome daśamaṃ gṛhṇāti |*), Ai. Brā. 3-1. And elsewhere it is recited: दशैतानध्वर्युः प्रातःसवने ग्रहान् गृह्णातीति (*daśaitānadhvaryuḥ prātaḥsavane grahān gṛhṇātīti*). With this in view the epithet दशयंत्रं (*daśayaṃtraṃ*) is used.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अयं (*ayaṃ*)—this Soma
- द्यावापृथिवी (*dyāvāpṛthivī*)—heaven and earth
- वि ष्कभायत् (*vi ṣkabhāyat*)—set up individually
- अयं (*ayaṃ*)—this Soma
- रथं (*rathaṃ*)—the chariot of Sūrya
- सप्तरश्मिं (*saptaraśmiṃ*)—as one having seven rays (rays or wheels)

### Printed p. 132

- आयुनक् (*āyunak*)—yoked
- अयं सोमः (*ayaṃ somaḥ*)—this same Soma
- गोषु अंतः (*goṣu aṃtaḥ*)—in the bodies of the cows
- शच्या (*śacyā*)—by his intelligence in the form of resolve
- उत्सं (*utsaṃ*)—that which flows forth
- दशयंत्रं (*daśayaṃtraṃ*)—that which consists of ten instruments
- पक्वं (*pakvaṃ*)—and ripened
- दाधार (*dādhāra*)—has filled with milk

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

This Soma set up heaven and earth individually. This same Soma made the chariot of Sūrya one with seven rays. And by his intelligence in the form of resolve he has filled the bodies of the cows with milk that flows forth, consists of ten instruments, and is ripened.

##### English translation
This (Soma) has fixed heaven and earth: this has harnessed the seven-rayed chariot (of the sun): this Soma has developed of its own will the mature deeply-organized secretion in the cows.

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

The supplementary mantras (pariśiṣṭa) to be recited at the end of this sūkta are given at the end of this Maṇḍala.

## Sūkta 45 (Maṇḍala 6)

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> य आनयदिति त्रयस्त्रिंशद्वचं द्वितीयं सूक्तं बृहस्पतिपुत्रस्य शंयोरार्षं ।
> पुरूतममित्येषातिनिच्छत् त्रयः सप्तकाः पादनिच्छत् मध्यमः षट्कश्चेदतिनिच्छदित्युक्तलक्षणात् त्रयस्त्रिंश्यनुष्टुप् शिष्टा गायत्र्यः । अधि बृबुः पणीनामित्यस्मिन्नंत्ये
> तृचे बृबुर्नाम तक्षा स्सूयते । अतः स तृचस्तद्देवताकः । शिष्टास्त्रिंशद्यॆंद्रः ।
> तथा चानुक्रांतं । य आनयत्रयस्त्रिंशद्दायत्रं पुरूतममतिनिच्छदंत्यानुष्टुप्
> तृचेऽंत्ये बृबुस्तक्षा दैवतमिति ॥ महाव्रतेऽपि निष्केवल्येऽंत्यतृचवर्जमेतत्तूक्तं । तथैव पंचमारण्यकं । य अनयत्परावत इति तिस्र उत्तमा उद्दरति ॥
> ऐ. आ. ५-२-५ । इति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The second sūkta, of thirty-three Ṛks, beginning य आनयत् (*ya ānayat*), has Śaṃyu, son of Bṛhaspati, as its seer. By the stated definition, the verse पुरूतमं (*purūtamaṃ*) is atinicṛt (when there are three sevens it is pādanicṛt, and if the middle one is a six it is atinicṛt); the thirty-third is an anuṣṭubh, the remaining ones gāyatrī. In the last triplet, अधि बृबुः पणीनां (*adhi bṛbuḥ paṇīnāṃ*), the carpenter named Bṛbu is praised; so that triplet has him as its deity, and the remaining thirty have Indra. So the Anukramaṇī says: य आनयत्रयस्त्रिंशद्दायत्रं पुरूतममतिनिच्छदंत्यानुष्टुप् तृचेऽंत्ये बृबुस्तक्षा दैवतमिति ॥ (*ya ānayatrayastriṃśaddāyatraṃ purūtamamatinicchadaṃtyānuṣṭup tṛce 'ṃtye bṛbustakṣā daivatamiti ‖*). In the Mahāvrata too, in the niṣkevalya, this sūkta is used, except for the last triplet. So also the fifth Āraṇyaka: य अनयत्परावत इति तिस्र उत्तमा उद्दरति (*ya anayatparāvata iti tisra uttamā uddarati*), Ai. Ā. 5-2-5.

##### Anuvāda

This sūkta, य आनयत् (*ya ānayat*), is the second sūkta in the fourth Anuvāka. It has thirty-three Ṛks. The seer of this sūkta is Śaṃyu, son of Bṛhaspati. The verse in this sūkta beginning पुरूतमं (*purūtamaṃ*) (the twenty-ninth Ṛk) is in the metre called Atinicṛt. A verse with three feet of seven syllables each is said to be of the Pādanicṛt metre. If the first and the last feet have seven syllables each and the middle foot has six syllables, it is called the Atinicṛt metre. The thirty-third Ṛk is in the Anuṣṭup metre. The remaining Ṛks are in the Gāyatrī metre. Because in the last three Ṛks, beginning अधि बृबुः पणीनां (*adhi bṛbuḥ paṇīnāṃ*), the carpenter named Bṛbu is praised, Bṛbu is the deity of those three Ṛks; Indra is the deity of the remaining thirty Ṛks; in the Anukramaṇī it is said: य आनयत्पयस्त्रंशद्दायत्रं पुरूतममतिनिच्छदंत्यानुष्टुप् तृचेऽन्तै बृबुस्तक्षा दैवतं (*ya ānayatpayastraṃśaddāyatraṃ purūtamamatinicchadaṃtyānuṣṭup tṛce 'ntai bṛbustakṣā daivataṃ*) **NOTE**. In the Mahāvratestiʻ **NOTE** the remaining Ṛks of this sūkta, leaving out the last three Ṛks, are used for the niṣkevalya-śastra mantras; this is indicated in the fifth Āraṇyaka of the Aitareya-Āraṇyaka by the sentence य आनयत्पारावत इति तिस्त्र उत्तमा उद्धरति (*ya ānayatpārāvata iti tistra uttamā uddharati*) (Ai. Ā. 5-2-5).

##### Particulars

Sūkta 45. Maṇḍala 6. Anuvāka 4. Aṣṭaka 4. Adhyāya 7. Vargas 21, 22, 23, 24, 25, 26. Number of Ṛks in the sūkta: 33. Seer: Śaṃyu Bārhaspatya. Deity: 1-30 Indra; 31-33 Bṛbu Takṣan. Metre: 1-28, 30-32 Gāyatrī; 29 Atinicchat; 33 Anustup.

### Printed p. 133

#### Ṛk 1

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> य आन॑यत्परा॒वतः॒ सुनी॑ती तु॒र्वशं॒ यदुं॑ ।  
> इंद्रः॒ स नो॒ युवा॒ सखा॑ ॥ १ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> यः । आ । अनयत् । परावतः । सुनीती । तुर्वशं । यदुं ॥  
> इंद्रः । सः । नः । युवा । सखा ॥ १ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> य इंद्रस्तुर्वशं यदुं चैतत्संज्ञा राजानौ शत्रुभिर्दूरदेशे प्रक्षिप्तौ
> सुनीति सुनीत्या शोभनेन नयनेन परावतस्तस्माद्दूरदेशादानयत् अनीतवान्
> युवा तरुणः स इंद्रो नोऽस्माकं सखा भवतु ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The Indra who brought, by good guidance, from a distant place the two kings named Turvaśa and Yadu, who had been thrown into a far country by enemies, may that youthful Indra be our friend.

### Printed p. 134

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- यः (*yaḥ*)—the Indra who
- तुर्वशं (*turvaśaṃ*)—the one named Turvaśa
- यदुं (*yaduṃ*)—and the one named Yadu, the two kings
- सुनीती (*sunītī*)—by his excellent guidance
- परावशः (*parāvaśaḥ*)—from a very distant country
- आ आनयत् (*ā ānayat*)—brought
- युवा (*yuvā*)—youthful
- स इंद्रः (*sa iṃdraḥ*)—that same Indra
- नः (*naḥ*)—to us
- सखा (*sakhā*)—let him be a friend

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

The Indra who brought the two kings named Turvaśa and Yadu from a very distant country by his excellent guidance, may that same youthful Indra be a friend to us also.

##### English translation
May that youthful Indra, who by good guidance, brought Turvasa and Yadu from afar, (be) our friend.

#### Ṛk 2

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अ॒वि॒प्रे चि॒द्वयो॒ दध॑दना॒शुना॑ चि॒दर्व॑ता ।  
> इंद्रो॒ जेता॑ हि॒तं धनं॑ ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अविप्रे । चित् । वयः । दधत् । अनाशुना । चित् । अर्वता ॥  
> इंद्रः । जीता । हितं । धनं ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> अविप्रे चित् । विप्रः स्तोता । तद्विलक्षणेऽपि पुरुषे वयोऽन्नं दधत् ।
> इंद्रो धारयति । किमु वक्तव्यं स्तोतरि धारयतीति । स इंद्रोऽनाशुना चित्
> अक्षिप्रगमनेनाप्यर्वताश्वेन हितं शत्रुषु निहितं स्थापितं धनं जेता जयशीलो
> भवति ॥ जयतेस्ताच्छेलिकस्तृन् । अतो न लोकाव्ययेति षष्ठिप्रतिषेधः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Even on one who is अविप्रे (*avipre*), not a विप्रः (*vipraḥ*), a praiser, Indra bestows वयः (*vayaḥ*), food; what need to say that he bestows it on the praiser? That Indra, even with a horse that is not swift-going, becomes the winner of the wealth that has been deposited among the enemies.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- इंद्रः (*iṃdraḥ*)—Indra
- अविप्रे चित् (*avipre cit*)—even to a man who is not a praiser
- वयः (*vayaḥ*)—food
- दधत् (*dadhat*)—gives
- अनाशुना अर्वता चित् (*anāśunā arvatā cit*)—even though he goes with a horse that does not move swiftly

### Printed p. 135

- हितं (*hitaṃ*)—deposited among the enemies
- धनं (*dhanaṃ*)—wealth
- जेता (*jetā*)—he brings under his own control

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

Indra gives food even to a man who is not a praiser. Even when he goes with a horse that does not move swiftly, he brings under his own control the wealth deposited among the enemies.

##### English translation
Indra gives sustenance, even to the undevout : he is the conqueror of wealth accumulated (by enemies), through (going against) them with a slow-paced steed.

#### Ṛk 3

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> म॒हीर॑स्य॒ प्रणी॑तयः पू॒र्विरु॒त प्रश॑स्तयः ।  
> नास्य॑ क्षीयंत ऊ॒तयः॑ ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> महीः । अस्य । प्रनीतयः । पूर्विः । उत । प्रशस्तयः ।  
> न । अस्य । क्षियंते । ऊतयः ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> अस्येंद्रस्य प्रणीतयः प्रकृष्टा नीशयः प्रापणानि महीर्मह्यो महत्यो
> भवंति । उतापि च प्रशस्तयः प्रकृष्टाः स्तुतयः स्तोतृभिः क्रियमाणाः
> पूर्वीर्बह्यो भवंति । तथास्येंद्रस्योतयो रक्षा न क्षीयंते । नापचीयंते
> किंतु सर्वदा वर्धंत एव ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The प्रणीतयः (*praṇītayaḥ*) of this Indra, his excellent leadings, his ways of bringing about, are great; and likewise the प्रशस्तयः (*praśastayaḥ*), the excellent praises made by the praisers, are many. And so the ऊतयः (*ūtayaḥ*), the protections of this Indra, do not decay, do not diminish, but always only increase.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अस्य (*asya*)—of Indra
- प्रणीतयः (*praṇītayaḥ*)—the excellent and skilful policies
- महीः (*mahīḥ*)—are great
- उत (*uta*)—and
- प्रशस्तयः (*praśastayaḥ*)—the acts in the form of praise
- पूर्वीः (*pūrvīḥ*)—are many
- अस्य (*asya*)—of that same Indra
- ऊतयः (*ūtayaḥ*)—the protections
- न क्षीयंते (*na kṣīyaṃte*)—do not come to destruction

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

The excellent and skilful policies of Indra are of the greatest majesty. The ways of praising him are also many. There is no destruction of Indra's protections.

### Printed p. 136

##### English translation
Vast are his designs, manifold are his praises, his protections are never withdrawn,

#### Ṛk 4

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> सखा॑यो॒ ब्रह्म॑वाह॒सेऽर्च॑त॒ प्र च॑ गायत ॥  
> स हि नः॒ प्रम॑तिर्म॒ही ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> सखायः । ब्रह्मवाहसे । अर्चत । प्र । च । गायत ॥  
> स । हि । नः । प्रमतिः । मही ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे सखायः समानख्याना। स्तोतारो ब्रह्मवाहसे ब्रह्मभिर्मंत्रैर्वहनीयाय प्राप्तव्यायेंद्रायार्चत । शस्त्राणि शंसत । प्र ग्रायत च स्तोत्राणि ।
> स हि स खल्विंद्रो नोऽस्माकं मही महती प्रमतिः प्रकृष्णा बुद्धिः । ईदृश्या
> बुद्धेः प्रदातेत्यर्थः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Friends, fellow praisers, offer worship to Indra, who is to be borne along and reached by the Brahmas, that is, by mantras: recite the śastras. And sing the stotras. For that same Indra is for us great and a प्रमतिः (*pramatiḥ*), an excellent understanding; the meaning is that he is the giver of such understanding.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- सखायः (*sakhāyaḥ*)—O praisers, friends
- ब्रह्मवाहसे (*brahmavāhase*)—to Indra, who is fit to be approached through praise alone
- अर्चत (*arcata*)—worship by reciting the śastra mantras
- प्र गायत (*pra gāyata*)—recite the praises
- स हि (*sa hi*)—that same Indra
- नः (*naḥ*)—to us
- मही (*mahī*)—possessing greatness
- प्रमतिः (*pramatiḥ*)—and excellent; he is the giver of intelligence

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O praisers, who are friends, recite the śastra mantras and worship Indra, who is fit to be approached through praise alone. Sing with stotras. That same Indra is for us the giver of an intelligence that is great and excellent.

##### English translation
Offer worship and praises, friends, to him who is to be attracted by prayers ; for he verily is our great intelligence.

### Printed p. 137

#### Ṛk 5

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> त्वमेक॑स्य वृत्रहन्नवि॒ता द्वयो॑रसि ।  
> उ॒तेदृशे॒ यथा॑ व॒यं ॥ ५ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> त्वं । एकस्य । वृत्रहन् । अविता । द्वयोः । असि ॥  
> उत । ईदृशे । यथा । वयं ॥ ५ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे वृत्रहन् वृत्राणां शत्रुणां हंतरिंद्र त्वमेकस्य स्तोतुर्द्वयोश्च
> स्तोत्रोरविता रक्षितासि । सर्वेषां स्तोतृणां रक्षको भवसीत्यर्थः । उतापि
> चेदृशे जनेऽप्यविता भवसि यथा यादृशा वयं त्वया रक्षिता बहुविधपुत्रपौत्रा
> अभूम । ईदृशस्यापि जनस्य त्वमेव रक्षको नान्य इत्यर्थः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Vṛtrahan, Indra, slayer of the Vṛtras, the enemies, you are the protector of one praiser and of two praisers; the meaning is that you are the protector of all praisers. And also of such a person as we are: as we, with sons and grandsons of many kinds, have been protected by you, so you are the protector of such a person too, and no one else is; this is the meaning.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- वृत्रहन् (*vṛtrahan*)—O Indra, destroyer of enemies
- त्वं (*tvaṃ*)—you
- एकस्य (*ekasya*)—for a praiser who is alone
- द्वयोः (*dvayoḥ*)—and for two praisers who are together
- अविता (*avitā*)—as protector
- असि (*asi*)—you are
- उत (*uta*)—and
- यथा (*yathā*)—as
- वयं (*vayaṃ*)—we who are your devotees
- ईदृते (*īdṛte*)—for people like us also you are protector

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, destroyer of enemies, whether your praiser is alone or two are together, you are the protector of all of them. You are the protector also of devotees like us, your praisers.

##### English translation
Slayer of Vritra, you are the protector of one (adorer), or of two, and of such as we are.

#### Ṛk 6

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> नय॒सीद्वति॒ द्विषः॑ कृ॒णोष्यु॑क्थशं॒सिनः॑ ।  
> नृभिः॑ सु॒वीर॑ उच्यसे ॥ ६ ॥

### Printed p. 138

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> नयसि । इत् । ऊं इति । अति । द्विषः । कृणोषि । उक्तशंसिनः ।  
> न्यभिः । सुवीरः । उच्यसे ॥ ६ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे इंद्रेत् त्वमेव द्विषो द्वेष्टनति नयसि । अस्मान्नयसि । तारयसि ।
> उ इति पूरकः । तथोक्तशंसिन उक्थानि शस्त्राणि शंसतः स्तोत्थानस्मान्हर्षि ।
> समृद्धान्करोषि । यस्मात्त्वं नृभिः स्तुतीनां नेतृभिः पुरुषैः सुवीरः शोभनैर्वीरैः पुत्रपौत्रादिभिः स्तोत्थभ्यो दातवैरुपेत इत्युच्यसे स्तूयसे अतस्तानप्यस्मभ्यं प्रयच्छेति शेषः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Indra, you alone lead the haters across and bring us over; उ (*u*) is a mere filler. Likewise you make prosperous us, the praisers who recite the uktha śastras. Since you are called सुवीरः (*suvīraḥ*), endowed with good heroes, sons, grandsons and the like, to be given to praisers, and are praised by men, the leaders of the praises, therefore give those same things to us also; this is what is to be supplied. **NOTE**

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- इत् (*it*)—you alone (O Indra)
- द्विषः (*dviṣaḥ*)—those who hate us
- अति नयसि (*ati nayasi*)—you drive far away from us
- उक्तशंसिनः (*uktaśaṃsinaḥ*)—those who praise you by reciting the uktha mantras
- कृणोषि (*kṛṇoṣi*)—you make prosperous
- सुवीरः (*suvīraḥ*)—you who favour with excellent heroic sons and the like
- नृभिः (*nṛbhiḥ*)—by the praisers
- उच्यसे (*ucyase*)—you are praised

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, it is you who drive far away from us those who hate us. You make prosperous those who praise you by reciting the uktha mantras. You, who favour with excellent heroic sons and the like, are praised by the praisers.

##### English translation
Remove (from us) those who hate us: prosper those who repeat your praise : bestower of excellent male descendants. you are glorified by men.

#### Ṛk 7

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> ब्र॒ह्माणं॒ ब्रह्म॑वाहसं गी॒र्भिः सखा॑यमृ॒ग्मियं॑ ।  
> गां न दो॒हसे॑ हुने ॥ ७ ॥

### Printed p. 139

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> ब्रह्माणं । ब्रह्मवाहसं । गीःभिः । सखायं । ऋग्मियं ।  
> गां । न । दोहसे । हुवे ॥ ७ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> ब्रह्माणं परिवृढं ब्रह्मवाहसं ब्रह्मभिः स्तुतिरूपैर्मंत्रैर्वहनीयं
> सखायमस्माकं सखिभूतमृग्मियं । ऋग्मा ऋचः । तदर्हं । स्तुत्यर्हमित्यर्थः ।
> एवंभूतमिंद्रं दोहसे दोग्धुं गां न गामिव गीर्भिः स्तुतिभिर्हुवे ।
> आह्वयाम्यस्मदीयान्कामान्नोग्धुं ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The great one; one who is borne along by Brahmas, that is, by mantras in the form of praise; a friend, one who has become a friend of ours; ऋग्मियं (*ṛgmiyaṃ*): ṛgmā means ṛcs, and he is worthy of them, that is, worthy of praise. Such an Indra I call with praises, as one calls a cow in order to milk her; I invite him in order to milk out our desires.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- ब्रह्माणं (*brahmāṇaṃ*)—the one of vast form
- ब्रह्मवाहसं (*brahmavāhasaṃ*)—who is borne along by praise
- सखायं (*sakhāyaṃ*)—who is a friend
- यग्नियं (*yagniyaṃ*)—who is worthy of praise: such an Indra
- गां न (*gāṃ na*)—as one milks a cow
- दोहसे (*dohase*)—to milk out the wealth of that Indra
- गीर्भिः (*gīrbhiḥ*)—with praises
- हुवे (*huve*)—I call

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

Indra, who is of the form of Brahman, who is borne along by praise, who is a friend and worthy of praise, I entreat and call with praises, to milk wealth from him as milk is drawn from a cow.

##### English translation
I invoke with hymns Indra, our friend, who is Brahma, who is attracted by prayer and entitled to adoration, to milk him as a cow.

#### Ṛk 8

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> यस्य॒ विश्वा॑नि॒ हस्त॑योरू॒चुर्वसू॑नि॒ नि द्वि॒ता ॥  
> वी॒रस्य॑ पृतना॒षहः॑ ॥ ८ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> यस्य । विश्वानि । हस्तयोः । ऊचु ॥ वसूनि । नि । द्विता ॥  
> वीरस्य । पृतनासहः ॥ ८ ॥

### Printed p. 140

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> वीरस्य वीर्यवतः पृतनासहः । पृतनाः शत्रुसेनाः । तासामभिभवितुर्यस्येंद्रस्य हस्तयोर्बाह्वर्विश्वानि सर्वाणि द्विता दिव्यपार्थिवरूपेण द्वैधं
> वर्तमानानि वसूनि विद्यंत इति न्यूचुः ऋषयो नितरां वदंति तमिंद्रं हुव इति
> पूर्वत्र संबंधः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

वीरस्य (*vīrasya*) means of the powerful one; पृतनासहः (*pṛtanāsahaḥ*): pṛtanās are enemy armies, and he is their overcomer. In whose hands, that is, in the arms of that Indra, all treasures, existing in a twofold way as heavenly and earthly, are found: so the seers say, they declare it constantly. That Indra I call; this is the connection with the previous verse.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- वीरस्य (*vīrasya*)—of the valiant one
- पृतनासहः (*pṛtanāsahaḥ*)—who defeats the armies of enemies
- यस्य (*yasya*)—of which Indra
- हस्तयोः (*hastayoḥ*)—in the arms
- विश्वानि (*viśvāni*)—all
- द्विता (*dvitā*)—that exist in two forms, heaven and earth
- वसूनि (*vasūni*)—treasures are established
- नि ऊचुः (*ni ūcuḥ*)—the seers have praised thus (that Indra I call)

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

I call with prayer that Indra, valiant and the defeater of the armies of enemies, in whose arms the seers have praised all treasures, existing in the two forms of heaven and earth, as established.
